# A773-2 PR B4: the screens, the display setting, Columns — task plan

**Goal.** PRs B1–B3 (#2381, #2392, #2406) built a dish that waits for the pass ("Requested"), every
service action on it, the pass's new board read and the pass's **Fire**. Nothing on a screen uses
them yet: the till still draws today's per-order pass from the three old reads. B4 puts the pass,
the pass monitor and the till's own Pass on the new board, with the owner's columns (Held, To fire,
In progress, Ready, and Overview off by default), Columns, and Fire; gives the station a **Held**
column that marks a dish **Waiting for the pass**; shows "at the pass" on the waiter's table screen;
builds the profile and device setting "show other stations' dishes on the pass"; retires the three
old reads; and builds the owner's later answer that one held dish fired on its own as a table's
first food is its «Nuevo pedido». Every station's "waits for the pass" setting stays false and has
no editor until PR C, so the only changes a venue sees before PR C are the new pass layout (with
nothing in a pass's view dimmed while no station it covers waits, D89, Owner question 2; and a
held dish no longer ages, so a held course's card no longer turns red, flashes, says Forgotten or
counts in the overdue badge as today's does, D68, D73), the station's held cards under Held and, after B4b,
the display setting. **The station's columns become choices** (revision 5, D94, the owner's answer to
Owner question 5, 2026-11-03 ~12:33): by default a station draws today's four columns, and a held
card sits at the bottom of **Waiting**, under a **Held** heading drawn only while something is held,
so a 1024 px station tablet looks as today whether or not anything is held. Columns lists every
column (Overview, Waiting + Held, Waiting, Held, Preparing, Ready), each with Show; Waiting and Held
as two separate columns are a choice for a screen wide enough, hidden at first. The screen turns to
tabs only when the columns switched on do not fit, as today, never because something became held.
**The pass takes the same shape in B4b** (revision 6, D95, the owner's answer to Owner question 6
part 2, 2026-11-03 ~16:05): by default the pass, the pass monitor and a till's All stations draw
**To fire**, **In progress** and **Ready**, with held cards at the bottom of To fire under a
**Held** heading, so a 1024 px pass tablet draws columns, held course or not; Columns names that
column **Held + To fire**, and Held and To fire as two separate columns are a choice, hidden at
first. The pass turns to its one-list layout only when the columns switched on do not fit.

**Words used here.** *Held*: both kitchen stamps null. *Requested*: `requested_at` set, `fired_at`
null (waiting for the pass). *Released*: `fired_at` set. *Card*: one `PassCard` of the new board
(B3 D45: one per party group across bills, or per bill for ungrouped dishes). *Informational*: an
item on a card that is not this pass's work (B3 D46, narrowed by D89), drawn dimmed. *In view*: an
item the pass's scope sees (`visible`, `passSees`). *Old reads*: `GET
/api/device/pass-screen`, `GET /api/device/pass-monitor`, `GET /api/expo/queue` and the server
functions only they use. *Pin*: a test of behaviour that is already right; every pin has a mutation
that turns it red.

**Implements.** The [slice plan](2026-11-01-a773-2b-requested-release.md)'s "B4 (outline)"
(B4.1–B4.8), every bullet of its "Left for B4" (B1's reviews, B2's plan, B3's plan), its D13 and
D15–D18; [B3's plan](2026-11-02-a773-2b3-pass-release.md)'s "Left for B4 (and later)" under its
D44–D65; [B2's plan](2026-11-01-a773-2b2-requested-lifecycle.md)'s "Left for B3 by this plan" points
that B3 passed on; the [master plan](2026-10-19-a773-2-firing-and-pass.md)'s Tasks 7a, 7b, 7 and the
till half of Task 5, Contracts 9–13, "Global Constraints", "Review Focus" and "Shared verification
contract"; the [spec](../specs/2026-10-16-a773-courses-and-groups-design.md) §5 and §13; and A797's
[design](../specs/2026-10-18-a797-device-screens-design.md) §§3.3–3.6 and §7, whose layouts the
master's dated amendment says A773-2 builds into. Decisions continue B3's numbering at D66.

**Researched at** `b0b5877ca6` (main, 2026-11-02; B3 landed as #2406, A1301 S11 as #2411). Every
file, line and signature below was read at that commit; the commands that were run are named where
they are used and listed in "Self-review". Implementers find code by function name, never by line
alone.

**Owner answers this plan applies** (2026-11-02, copy sheet and Questions 1–9 approved as written
~09:40; `~/waitron-campaign-c/a773-2b3-owner-questions.md`, outside the repository): Q1 the copy
sheet, every row; Q2 (a), Q4 (a), Q8 (a), Q9 (a) are built defaults (B2, B3); Q3 (b) and Q6 (b) were
built in B3 (D65, D57); Q5 (a) keeps «(en espera)» for another station's waiting dish on a cook's
ticket and the station screen's rest-of-order list (D86: already built); Q7 (a) names a table's first
send «Nuevo pedido». **Later answer (owner, 2026-11-02 ~15:50):** a waiter firing one held dish on
its own (`fireHeldLine`, "Fire now" on a single dish) as the table's first food gives its group
`new_order`, exactly as a whole held course does; no exception to the Q6 rule (D67). **B4's own
questions** (owner, 2026-11-02, `~/waitron-campaign-c/questions.md` "WATCHER 2026-11-02 ~19:45" and
"WATCHER 2026-11-02 ~19:55"): Owner questions 1–4 below, all as recommended — dimming (b) (D89), the
pass card help with the ~20:00 clause, the station card help (D90), the More menu's Pass help (D91),
«Curso listo» kept, and «Usar la configuración del perfil» kept over «Según el perfil». Owner
question 5 (2026-11-03 ~09:55, the station's Held column on a 1024 px tablet) was answered
2026-11-03 ~12:33 with a new shape (D94, superseding D93); Owner question 6 (what overlapping
columns show, the words that go with D94, and whether the pass follows the rule) was answered on
2026-11-03 in three relays ("WATCHER 2026-11-03 ~14:30", "~16:05" and "~16:10"): the station part
(a), its help texts and the Columns badge's sentence as recommended, and the station's two Waiting
headings left as they are, all already built by B1d1a–B1d4; and the pass part **(b)**, not the
recommendation, which is new work (D95, Tasks B1e1–B1e4).

**No location (owner, 2026-11-01, A1301; high priority).** Nothing here adds a `location_id`
column, a `locationId` parameter, or a per-location rule, setting, fixture or translation. The new
venue-service seat takes no `cfg` (slice plan D17); the Columns key uses the server's origin (slice
plan D16); existing `cfg` parameters stay as they are for A1301's S12a and S18 to remove.

**English only.** Spanish appears only in `apps/till/src/i18n/strings.ts`' `es` block,
`apps/till/src/i18n/help-strings.ts`, `apps/dashboard/src/i18n/strings.ts`' `es` block,
`apps/dashboard/src/i18n/alert-messages.ts` and `apps/server/src/guide/es/`. Tests read Spanish
from those files.

**Fiscal.** `packages/fiscal-verifactu/src/write-path.e2e.test.ts` and `src/inmutabilidad.test.ts`
are never edited and run unedited in B4a's Tasks A2, A2b and A19 and B4b's Tasks B5 and B13. No task touches
money, invoices, numbering, the hash chain or `adjustments-apply.ts`.

**Execution.** Every implementer is dispatched as `subagent_type: "implementer"` (medium effort),
one task per dispatch, in the PR's worktree. An implementer that passes about 150 calls with the
task unfinished stops at a passing or cleanly red point, commits, and hands over (done, left,
files, each check's state). Each task ends with the touched packages' `typecheck` clean, its
focused suites green, `pnpm format:check` clean, and a signed commit (`git commit -s`, explicit
paths staged, `git diff --cached --check`). No task is planned to end red. **Browser suites (till,
dashboard, venue-service `--project browser`) run in real headless Chromium: measure free memory
first (`memory_pressure | grep free`) and run named files, never a whole package.**

**The test-change rule (owner, 2026-10-05).** Adding fixture fields or rows is fine; a whole-shape
pin may gain the new keys. An existing check may change only where this plan, its spec or an owner
answer deliberately changes the behaviour it pins; it is changed to check the new behaviour at least
as strictly and is listed in "Changed test checks" with its `file:line`, the check before and after,
and the decision. Any other change to an existing assertion is a **stop** to flag, never made. A
guard under `scripts/` is never edited except the two profile-map header tables named below. Prefer
a new case to an edit.

## The cut: two pull requests

B4 is two pull requests, each landing on its own and each leaving `main` working.

| PR | Branch | What it ships | Why main still works |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **B4a** | `feat/a773-2b4-pass-screens` (this worktree) | The pass, the pass monitor and the till's own Pass on the new board: columns, cards, Columns, Overview, Fire, Ready levers; the till's All stations view on a new board route; a Requested dish aged from its request; a group card's party; the `fireHeldLine` first-food answer; the three old reads and their server functions, till methods and screen retired, their tests moved to the new board; the pass's guide; LOOK | Until a station the pass covers waits (PR C), nothing in the pass's view is dimmed and every dish in view counts for lateness (D89, built on Owner question 2's recommended (b)), so a default venue's pass reads as today's in the new columns, with today's late colours, overdue count and Forgotten mark for every dish that has been sent to be cooked. One difference every venue that holds courses sees: today's pass ages a held dish from when it was entered, so a held course's card goes red, flashes, says Forgotten and counts in the overdue badge (round-3 E3); on the new board a held dish has no age and does none of these (D68, D73). Once one waits, dishes of stations that do not are dimmed, as A797 §3.5 and Q51 (d) approve. No display setting exists yet, so none is without effect. The station screen and the table screen are unchanged |
| **B4b** | `feat/a773-2b4b-pass-display`, created off `main` after B4a landed (#2425) | The station's held cards under a Held heading at the bottom of Waiting by default, with every column, Waiting and Held apart included, a choice in Columns (D94: a 1024 px station still shows today's four columns whether or not anything is held), and "Waiting for the pass"; the pass's held cards at the bottom of To fire by default, with Held and To fire apart a choice in Columns (D95: a 1024 px pass draws columns whether or not anything is held); one helper for both screens' saved column layouts (B1c); the table screen's "at the pass"; department transfers' "waiting for the pass"; the closed-station alert's words for dishes that wait for the pass; the display setting end to end (venue-service columns and migration, the seat, the admin routes, `PUT /api/device/kitchen-screen-display`, the board's `display`, the dashboard's profile and device controls, the pass's Columns section); their guides; LOOK | The setting lands in the same PR as every client that reads or writes it (the dashboard's controls, the pass's Columns window, the board that applies it), which is the master's "do not land T7a's settings alone with mismatched clients" (`2026-10-19-a773-2-firing-and-pass.md:285`) |

Why not the outline's single PR: read against today's code it is about 46 implementer tasks (about
2,250 calls, after revision 2's splits); B3's 22 tasks in one PR were already the largest review this item has had. Why not the
cut the brief offered as an example (settings, migration, routes and dashboard first; screens
second): it lands a setting a manager can change in the dashboard while every pass screen ignores
it, which is exactly what the master forbids. Why this cut and not the reverse order: B4b's Columns
section and its board `display` edit the pass screen B4a builds. Each PR gets its own fresh-context
plan review, per-task reviews and `/finish-branch` (FULL path: B4a retires routes and changes a
cross-package contract; B4b carries a migration and a new device write route), with two Codex run-it
reviews each.

## What the planning pass found (at `b0b5877ca6`)

1. **The till reads only the old reads; the new ones have no screen.** `till-expo-screen.ts` (1,113
   lines) picks one of three reads in `#reload` (`apps/till/src/screens/till-expo-screen.ts:610-640`):
   `getExpoQueue` for board `"all"`, `getDevicePassScreen` for `"pass"`, `getDevicePassMonitor` for
   `"monitor"`, and draws one card per bill with course or group sections (`:814-860`). The new client
   methods `getDevicePassBoard`, `getDevicePassMonitorBoard`, `releaseDevicePass`
   (`apps/till/src/api/client.ts:3570-3593`) are called only by `client.test.ts:1927,1944`. At boot a
   kitchen display reads the old pass and monitor routes (`till-app.ts`, `#readKitchenScreen`,
   `:4486-4501`) and hands the answer to `<till-expo-screen>` (`#kitchenBoard`, `:19178-19216`).

2. **The till's own Pass has a third board: All stations.** A till whose device chose no pass or
   monitor opens its Pass drill (`till-app.ts:19424-19430`) on board `"all"` (`till-expo-screen.ts`,
   `#loadChoice`, `:557-575`), which reads `GET /api/expo/queue` (`till-api.ts:2318-2326`): every
   station and zone, checked by the session only (the profile-zones header,
   `till-api.profile-zones.test.ts:238-241`, says so). The new board routes need the device's pass or
   monitor screen (`device-api.ts:587-611`, `kitchenScreenOf`, `:1085-1099`), so retiring
   `/api/expo/queue` needs a replacement read for this view (D70).

3. **The new board never calls the old reader.** `readScopedPassBoard` (`pass-board.ts:121-139`) →
   `readCardRows` → `readPassRows` (`working-order.ts:8548-8738`); the release reads
   `readPassCardRows`. `readPassBoard` (`working-order.ts:8742`) has three production callers, all
   retiring: `listPassScreen` (`pass-board.ts:58-90`), `listPassMonitor` (`:93-118`) and
   `listExpoQueue` (`working-order.ts:8512-8514`). `ExpoItem` (`:8445`) must stay: `PassCardItem`
   extends it (`pass-card.ts`), and the till's `state/portion-groups.ts` reads its twin.
   `firedPassDishes` (the dark-pass alert, `pass-board.ts:243-283`) is independent. No alert, report
   or station read calls the old reader (searched: `rg -n "readPassBoard|listExpoQueue" apps packages
   -g '!*.test.ts'`).

4. **The old reads are probes in 26 server test files.** `rg -c` over `apps/server/src/*.test.ts`
   for the old functions and paths: `working-order.test.ts` 27 hits (its "listExpoQueue (KDS-3 …)"
   describe, `:6158-6402`, and bump tests, `:4810-5112`), `pass-board.test.ts` 20,
   `device-api.pass.test.ts` 20, `kitchen-allergy.reads.test.ts` 13, `pass-lifetime.test.ts` 10,
   `order-groups.test.ts` 9, then 20 files with 1–6 each (listed under Tasks A13a–A15b). On the till:
   `till-expo-screen.test.ts` (3,204 lines) and six siblings (`.a11y` 411, `.help` 404,
   `.help.a11y` 114, `.dispatch-retirement` 165, `.allergy` 121, `.kitchen-note` 120),
   `till-app-boot-and-counter.test.ts:304-1133`, `till-app.test.ts:508,15763-15769`,
   `test/equipment-app.ts:86`, `test/kitchen-allergy-fixtures.ts:2,72`,
   `kitchen-allergy.look.test.ts:52-55`, `kitchen-allergy-send.test.ts:158-159,323`,
   `client.test.ts:1853,1891,1912`. Revision 2 (`rg -n "till-expo-screen|TillExpoScreen" apps/till/src apps/till/test -g '!src/screens/till-expo-screen*'`):
   the element's tag or type is also asserted or mounted at `till-app.test.ts:13680,13687,13700,14430-14431,15766,15784,15798,15916,21255,21262,21269,21275,21841,21892`,
   `till-app-boot-and-counter.test.ts:336,558,650,661,677,731,734,769,812,818,868,1017,1069,1131,2590,6235,6242`,
   `screens/device-screens.phone-margin.test.ts:10,15,86`, `screens/kitchen-allergy.look.test.ts:7,25,57-58`
   and `screens/kitchen-allergy-send.test.ts:9-10,181`; and two comments in `api/client.ts`
   (`:1942`, `ExpoItem.band`; `:1994`, `ExpoOrder.worstBand`) name the old screen.

5. **A Requested dish on the board is aged from its insert.** `readPassRows` bands every row from
   `queued_at` (`working-order.ts:8690`), and `requestHeldItems` stamps a Requested row's
   `requested_at` and `pass_controlled` only (`apps/server/src/kitchen-release.ts:73`), so a dish held
   at 12:00 and fired at 12:30 reads `forgotten` at 12:31 (B3's review scratch S5). **Probe P3**
   (this worktree, reverted with `git checkout`): banding a row with `fired_at` null and
   `requested_at` set from `requested_at` instead, then
   `pnpm exec vitest run $(rg -l 'readPassRows|readScopedPassBoard|readPassBoard|listExpoQueue|listPassScreen|listPassMonitor|pass-board|pass-monitor|expo/queue|band' src -g '*.test.ts')`
   in `apps/server` → `Test Files 141 passed (141)`, `Tests 6307 passed | 6 skipped`. So no test pins
   a Requested item's band today; Task A2 adds the case. What a failing probe would have printed: a red
   naming `band`, meaning a pin to change. The station queue's band (`:8438`) needs no change: a card
   with no released dish is untimed there (`apps/till/src/state/kitchen-cards.ts`, `since`).

6. **A group card's `party` is its first row's bill's party.** `buildPassBoard` takes `party` from
   the first row with a bill party (`pass-card.ts:245-255`), not the group's own party
   (`target.partyId`, `:218`). After a merge that leaves a paid bill on the closed party, the card's
   Ready lever would send the closed party's revision (B3's "Left for B4"). The group's party is a
   row field already (`groupPartyId`, `working-order.ts:8108`); its revision is not read (D69).

7. **`fireHeldLine` starts a `next_course` group whatever came before.** `order-groups.ts:613`
   passes `"next_course"` to `startGroup`; `releaseGroup` reads `nextFireKind` and writes `new_order`
   for a party with no fired group (`:878-883`). **Probe P2** (reverted): reading `nextFireKind`
   before `startGroup` in `fireHeldLine` and passing `new_order` when it answers so, then running the
   28 server files naming `request_kind|requestKind|new_order|nextFireKind|fireHeldLine|fire_line|/fire`
   → `Tests 1 failed | 2054 passed (2055)`; the one red is
   `order-groups.request-kind.test.ts` "gives Fire now on a held dish a next_course group"
   (`:134-146`), introduced by B1 (#2381, `git log -S`). What a failing probe would have printed: a
   red in any other file.

8. **The station has no Held column; a held card sits in Waiting.** `STATION_COLUMNS` is Overview,
   Waiting, Preparing, Ready (`apps/till/src/state/kitchen-columns.ts:17-21`); the widget draws only
   `DRAWN` keys (`widgets/station-queue.ts:82-98`) and skips `held` ("leaves out the columns it
   cannot draw yet", `station-queue.test.ts:3842`). A card with no released dish is drawn dashed in
   Waiting with "Held, not released" (`:1366-1405`; A797 build plan D3: "A held card stays in
   Waiting until A773-2 adds Held"). The till's `StationQueueItem` twin has no `phase`,
   `requestedAt`, `readyAt` or `passControlled` (the server's has them since B3.3,
   `working-order.ts:7869-7905`). Read, not run: four station-queue cases pin today's placement
   (Changed test checks, B4b).

9. **The waiter's screens show nothing, or "Fired", for a Requested dish.** `#rowState`
   (`till-table-order-screen.ts:4402-4410`) returns nothing when `kitchen.firedAt` is null; the Sent
   list's `#status` (`widgets/sent-orders.ts:510-526`) says "Fired"/«Marchado» when `row.firedAt` is
   null. `CurrentOrderKitchen` and `CurrentOrderRow` (`apps/till/src/api/client.ts:591,605`) lack
   B3.3's `requestedAt` and `phase`.

10. **The closed-station alert says "went to" for a dish waiting for the pass.**
    `rerouteHeldAtRelease` (`station-move.ts:35-174`) collects stranded rows and calls
    `raiseReleasedAtClosedStation` (`closed-station-alert.ts:24-60`) with code
    `route.released_at_closed_station`, worded in `apps/dashboard/src/i18n/alert-messages.ts:408-411`.
    It runs before `requestHeldItems`, so it cannot see which rows become Requested, but the stations'
    setting is readable (`passWaitingStations`, `kitchen-release.ts`). Two committed cases assert this
    code with a waiting station: `pass-release.test.ts` "fires a split-off extra at its closed station,
    which no move can free" (`:1144`, alert checked at `:1168-1172`, `:1204`) and "raises the
    closed-station alert at the waiter's fire, and never again (Grill waits: %s)" (`:1207-1252`, the
    `true` row). Their helper `closedStationAlerts` filters by the old code (`:808-814`).
    `scripts/personal-log-params.test.ts:129` lists the old code's `orderLabel`; a new code without
    `orderLabel` needs no row there.

11. **The display setting's storage is plain ADD COLUMN, and its writers replace whole rows.**
    **Probe P1** (this worktree, reverted with `git checkout` and the two generated files removed;
    `git status --short` clean after): `showNonPassStations: flag("show_non_pass_stations").notNull().default(true)`
    on `deviceProfileKitchenScreens` and `flag("show_non_pass_stations")` (nullable) on
    `deviceKitchenScreens` (`packages/venue-service/src/schema/kitchen-screens.ts:26-50,116-137`), then
    `pnpm db:generate` in `packages/venue-service` (drizzle-kit `^0.31.11`) wrote
    `0073_cuddly_outlaw_kid.sql`: `ALTER TABLE \`device_kitchen_screens\` ADD \`show_non_pass_stations\` integer;`
    and `ALTER TABLE \`device_profile_kitchen_screens\` ADD \`show_non_pass_stations\` integer DEFAULT true NOT NULL;`
    — no rebuild, so no inbound key is touched. Then
    `pnpm exec vitest run scripts/schema-constraints.test.ts scripts/migrations-match-schema.test.ts scripts/two-file-foreign-keys.test.ts scripts/classification-complete.test.ts scripts/migration-upgrade.test.ts scripts/journal-monotonic.test.ts`
    → `Test Files 6 passed`, `Tests 56 passed`: `migration-upgrade` needed no `CANDIDATES` entry
    (a flag takes the generic values, `scripts/migration-upgrade.test.ts:634-686`). In `apps/server`,
    `src/configuration-transfer.test.ts` and `scripts/demo-seed/seed.madrid-baseline.test.ts` →
    `Tests 3 failed | 150 passed`: both Madrid locales (`before/device_kitchen_screens: … columns …(4) …
    to deeply equal …(3)`) and "round-trips deleted kitchen configuration and preserves archived
    products and empty profile bounds" (`:7273-7279`, a whole-row pin). In `packages/venue-service`,
    `--project node src/kitchen-screens.test.ts src/configuration-transfer.test.ts src/migrations.test.ts`
    → `Tests 355 passed`. What a failing probe would have printed: a `__new_device_…` rebuild in the
    SQL, or `migration-upgrade` refusing the step. The writers matter more than the DDL: the profile
    writer deletes and reinserts every row of a profile (`replaceProfileKitchenScreens`,
    `packages/venue-service/src/kitchen-screens.ts:157-200`, rows built by `insertScreen`, `:251-256`),
    and `setDeviceKitchenScreens` does the same for a device (`:537-568`), so the new column would be
    reset on every save unless both carry it (D82).

12. **What already exists for the words.** Read in `apps/till/src/i18n/strings.ts` (EN block, ES from
    `:1872`): Overview `station.overview` (`:692`/«Resumen» `:2550`), Columns and its move/show words
    (`:694-699`/`:2552-2557`), `station.group_held` (`:705`/«En espera, sin marchar»),
    `station.elsewhere_held` (`:708`/«En espera»), the stale-screen sentence `station.table_changed`
    (`:717`/`:2575`), the state words (`:718-720`/«Esperando», «Preparando», «Listo»), Fire
    `table.group_fire` («Marchar», `:2987`), "Ready {n} min" `table.row_ready_ago` (`:1105`/«Listo {n}
    min» `:2947`), Hide `find_page.hide` (`:648`/«Ocultar» `:2509`). Dashboard
    (`apps/dashboard/src/i18n/strings.ts`, ES from `:3748`): `table.show_column`/`table.hide_column`
    («Mostrar»/«Ocultar», `:796-797`/`:4558-4559`). None of the approved sheet's new phrases is in
    either file yet (`/usr/bin/grep` for each Spanish phrase matched nothing). The help text
    `help.pass.card` (`apps/till/src/i18n/help-strings.ts:58-59`, ES `:120-121`) says "One card per
    order … The minutes count from when the order was opened", which the new board makes false (D77).
    Revision 2, read in the same file: `help.more.pass` (`:45-46`, ES `:107-108`) says the pass
    shows "the open orders, each with its dishes grouped by course, or by group on a table's bill",
    false after B4a (D91, Owner question 4); `help.pass.cards_part` "Order cards" / «Tarjetas de
    pedido» (`:60`, `:122`) names per-order cards (D77 reuses `help.kitchen.cards_part`); and
    `help.kitchen.card` (`:49`, ES `:111`) says a card sits "in the column of its least advanced
    dish" and "A card with a dashed border is held until a waiter requests cooking", both false
    after B4b's D78 (D90, Owner question 3).

13. **A797's hand-off.** A797's slice 9 built the Columns store (`kitchen-columns.ts`), 9b the Columns
    window (`widgets/kitchen-columns-dialog.ts`), 9c Overview (`state/kitchen-overview.ts`,
    `overviewRows`) and the measured narrow fit (`state/kitchen-board-fit.ts`, `columnsFit`). Its
    build plan D3 places a missing registered key at its default position with its default hidden and
    collapsed state, and D15 expects A773-2 to add `held` to `STATION_COLUMNS` and build the pass's
    scopes (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-24-a797-device-screens-build.md:7210-7240,7320-7330`). Its
    slice 12b is "short names on pass cards, and Overview on the pass if A773-2 shipped without it"
    (`:266`): B4a builds Overview, so 12b keeps only short names. A797 design §3.5 draws the pass below
    about 1000 px as "sections of one list, in the same order, each collapsible by its heading", with
    Columns as a button in the pass's own header on a till.

14. **At a venue where no station waits, B3's board marks every dish informational** (review-1 C1).
    `cardItem` (`apps/server/src/pass-card.ts:170-190`) sets `informational: !visible || !passWork`,
    with `passWork` from `stationWaitsNow` (held) or `passControlled` (initiated); until PR C both are
    false for every dish, so every dish of every card is informational. The review's scratch Z1 read
    `"informational":true` and `overviewOwn: 0` for a fired burger at a venue where Grill does not
    wait. **Probe P4** (this worktree, reverted with `git checkout --` and the scratch file deleted;
    `git status --short` clean after; memory 94% free): in `buildPassBoard` a board-level flag
    `dims = aStationInViewWaits || live.some((row) => passSees(scope, row) && passWork(row))`, with
    `passWork` replaced by `!dims || passWork`, and `readScopedPassBoard` reading
    `aStationInViewWaits` as "any `kitchen_stations` row with `waits_for_pass` among the scope's
    stations (every station when `stationIds` is null)". Then
    `env -u AI_AGENT -u CLAUDECODE pnpm exec vitest run --maxWorkers=8` over the 14 server files
    naming `informational|readScopedPassBoard|buildPassBoard|pass-board|pass-monitor-board|releasePassItems|pass-screen/release`
    → `Test Files 1 failed | 13 passed (14)`; the one red is `pass-board.requested.test.ts` "shows a
    held group as a held card with nothing to fire" (`:456-483`), whose second half (`:477-481`)
    expects the held burger `informational: true` after Grill stops waiting. A scratch case over
    `setupSplitExtrasVenue` (a fired burger read 25 minutes later) printed
    `"informational":false,"band":"forgotten"` and `overviewOwn: 1` with no station waiting;
    `"informational":true` and `overviewOwn: 0` with Fryer waiting and the pass on every station (the
    control); and `"informational":false` with Fryer waiting but the pass covering Grill and Bar only.
    What a failing probe would have printed: `"informational":true` with no station waiting, or a red
    in `pass-card.test.ts` (none: its fixtures all hold pass work).

## Scope

**In B4a.** `fireHeldLine`'s first food (D67); a Requested item's board age (D68); a group card's
party (D69); dimming only once the pass controls something (D89); `GET /api/expo/board` for the
till's All stations view (D70); the pass's Columns defaults and scopes (D72); a pure pass view
(`state/pass-view.ts`), a board widget (`widgets/pass-board.ts`) and a new screen
(`screens/till-pass-screen.ts`) for the pass, the monitor and All stations (D71, D73–D75); the till
app on the new screen and boards, with the `act-as-kds` mount gate tested; the old screen's tests
moved to the new one; the old reads, their server functions, client methods, types and screen
retired with every probe moved (D76); `help.pass.card` (D77) and `help.more.pass` (D91); the pass's
guide in English and Spanish; the developer docs and backlog citations of the retired code; LOOK.

**In B4b.** The till's twins of B3.3's fields; the station's held cards and its column choices (D78,
D94); the pass's held cards under To fire and its column choices (D95); one helper for both screens' saved column layouts (B1c, B4a's backlog hand-off); "at the pass" on the
table screen and Sent list (D79); the closed-station alert for waiting dishes (D80); department
transfers' request time (D81); the display setting: columns, seat, effective value, admin parsers,
`PUT /api/device/kitchen-screen-display`, the board's `display`, the dashboard's profile and device
controls, the pass's Columns section (D82–D85); `help.kitchen.card` (D90); guides; LOOK.

**Out of B4.** The station setting's editor, its import validation and the coverage warning (PR C).
Ticket headings, "Goes with kitchen food" and removing `away_at`, `pass_item_marks` and
`fire_control` (PR D). Short names on pass cards (A797 slice 12b). Help badges for the new columns
and Fire (backlog, "Left for later"). The pass header's line naming what the pass covers (A797
§3.5 "The header names the pass and what it covers"; master T7 "Keep pass identity/coverage
header"): B4a keeps the identity today's screen has (the device's name as title on a display, the
Pass title and Back on a till, D71) and leaves the coverage line to the backlog, because it needs
new words.

**Backlog.** B4a's last task rewrites the A773-2 entry (`docs/backlog.md:88-95`, and
`docs/backlog/till.md` "A773-2. Firing and the pass", `:2516-2560`) to say B4a landed, deletes the
"B4 builds one owner answer" and "Before PR C: the expo board" paragraphs (both done), and links this
plan; and edits A797's entry (`docs/backlog.md:140-153`) so slice 12b is short names only. It also
rewrites "Pin two pass-board rules no test holds" (`docs/backlog.md:2559-2562`) to keep only the
switched-off zone half (`passScopeOf`): the course-section half names `readPassBoard`'s course
sections, which retire (D76), so it is deleted, not kept. It moves A1250's pointer
(`docs/backlog.md:1979`, `till-expo-screen.ts` `.order.age-warm`) to the new widget (D73 keeps the
pass's colours). It adds one entry: the manager overview's "orders taking too long" ages a dish
waiting for the pass, and a held one, from its insert (`packages/reporting/src/overdue-orders.ts`,
`readOverdueOrders`, selects `ticketItems.queuedAt` with no `firedAt` or `requestedAt` filter,
read at `:47-70`), the same class as D68 on a report; I believe the held half predates the branch
(not blamed line by line). And the pass header's coverage line (Out of B4). B4b's last task says PR
B is complete and PR C is next, and adds the follow-ups under "Left for later".

**Venue reset.** None. B4a has no migration; B4b's adds two columns with plain ADD COLUMN (finding
11). Each PR's first line says so. This departs from the master's "Schema releases require a
pre-live reset, stated first in the PR" (`2026-10-19-a773-2-firing-and-pass.md:30`): see
Departures.

## Decisions

D1–D20 (slice plan), D21–D43 (B2) and D44–D65 (B3) bind unchanged, except B3 D46's
`informational`, which D89 narrows. These are B4's, dated 2026-11-02 (D89–D91 added by revision 2;
D93, dated 2026-11-03, by revision 4; D94, dated 2026-11-03, by revision 5, superseding D93; D95,
dated 2026-11-03, by revision 6, superseding D72's column sentences and D94's note on the pass).

- **D66 Two pull requests** ("The cut"). B4a lands first; B4b is planned here in full and gets its
  own fresh-context plan review when it starts, after rebasing this plan's B4b tasks onto B4a's merge.

- **D67 One held dish fired as the table's first food is its New order** (owner, 2026-11-02 ~15:50).
  `fireHeldLine` reads `nextFireKind(tx, partyId)` before `startGroup` and passes `new_order` when it
  answers `new_order`, else `next_course`. Read before the insert, because the new group is fired and
  would itself count. B1's case at `order-groups.request-kind.test.ts:134-146` changes (Changed test
  checks); a new case pins `next_course` when the party already has a fired group.

- **D68 A Requested item on the pass board ages from its request.** In `readPassRows` the band and
  the item's `queuedAt` field stay as they are for every row except one with `fired_at` null and
  `requested_at` set, whose band is measured from `requested_at`. A released row keeps `queued_at`
  (the release time, B3 D49), so a station's age and the pass's agree once the pass fires; a held row
  keeps its insert time (it shows no age on a Held card). This is B3's "B4 must fix before PR C"
  (option one of two; the other, stamping `queued_at` at the request, would also move the station
  queue's order and age for Requested rows, which D2 leaves to the release).
  **The till measures with the same rule, on its own clock** (revision 2, review-1 I1). Today's
  screen ignores the server's `band` and re-derives every item's band from `queuedAt` on each render
  with its ticking clock (`till-expo-screen.ts`, `#itemBand` and `#clockNow`, `:1070-1076`; the
  Forgotten mark and portion key read it in `#rows` and `#item`, `:940-956`), so bands climb between
  refreshes. The new view keeps that behaviour and the D68 rule: `passItemBand(item, now)` in
  `state/pass-view.ts` measures from `requestedAt` when `phase === "requested"`, otherwise from
  `queuedAt`, with `classifyBand` and the item's `thresholds`; a `held` item has no band (it is not
  cooking, and D68 keeps its insert time, so banding it would call a long-held dish forgotten; the
  station's held card is untimed the same way, `kitchen-cards.ts` `since`). `passCardViews` takes
  `now`; the screen passes its `TickingClock`'s time, as today. The server's `band` stays correct
  for any other reader and is not read by the till.

- **D69 A group card's party is the group's own party.** `readPassRows` reads the revision of the
  group's party (a correlated subquery on `order_groups.party_id`, beside `partyFirstGroupAt`), and
  `buildPassBoard` sets `party: { id: group.partyId, revision }` on a group card. A bill card has no
  `party`, as today. So a group card after a merge names the surviving party, whose revision the
  group Ready needs.

- **D70 The till's All stations view reads `GET /api/expo/board`.** Same admission as
  `/api/expo/queue` (a signed-in session, nothing else; `requireSession`, `till-api.ts:2320`): the
  board over every station and zone (`{ stationIds: null, zoneIds: null }`) with the release stripped
  (`withoutRelease`, moved from `device-api.ts:1067-1077` to `pass-board.ts` and exported), answered
  as `{ board }`. As built in B4a, the route passes `{ release: false }` to `readScopedPassBoard`
  (`apps/server/src/till-api.ts:2320`) and `withoutRelease` is gone (revision 4, review M2). A till with no pass screen has no release authority (`releasePassItems` refuses
  without an available pass screen, B3 D48), so it shows To fire cards without Fire, as a monitor
  does, and keeps today's Ready levers. Its `display` is the default (show) in B4a and stays so in
  B4b: it has no kitchen-screen row to carry a policy. No zone check is added: today's read has none
  and changing that is not B4's (the profile-zones header keeps saying so).

- **D71 A new screen replaces `till-expo-screen`.** `screens/till-pass-screen.ts` (element
  `till-pass-screen`) owns reads, refresh, fencing, Columns, Fire and Ready; `widgets/pass-board.ts`
  (`till-pass-board`) is a pure view of a `PassBoard` that emits events, as `till-station-queue` is
  for a station; `state/pass-view.ts` holds the pure decisions (D73–D75) so they are tested without a
  browser layout. The old screen and its tests stay untouched until the new one carries every
  behaviour they pin (Tasks A11a–A12b), so every commit is green. Building in place would leave a task
  red while the old tests still mock the old reads.
  **What the new element keeps** (revision 2, review-1 I2), so the app and allergy suites keep
  their assertions apart from the tag: the `back-to-counter` event and its Back button
  (`expo.back`, `data-back`), the `runsPass` property, the `embedded` property and the app's
  `slot="drill"`, no `fireControl` property, `deviceName` as the title on a display and `expo.title`
  on a till (`till-expo-screen.ts:742-749`), the `device-unauthorized` and `kitchen-screen-changed`
  events, and the DOM hooks `data-item`, `data-forgotten`, `data-note`, `data-item-allergens`,
  `data-item-customisation`, `data-kitchen-note`, `data-lever-error`, `data-table-changed`,
  `data-stale`, `data-unavailable`, `data-reprint`, `data-ready` (valued by course id) and
  `data-group-ready` (valued by group id), plus `data-kitchen-allergy` from the shared allergy block.
  Retired with per-bill cards and sections (D76): `data-order`, `data-course`, `data-group-section`,
  `data-group-held`; the new card carries `data-card="<key>"` and each column `data-column="<key>"`.
  Only the tag and type names change in the app and allergy suites (Changed test checks).

- **D72 Columns for the pass.** `PASS_COLUMNS = { order: ["overview", "held", "toFire",
  "inProgress", "ready"], hidden: ["overview"], collapsed: [] }` for the pass, the monitor and All
  stations (Overview off is the owner's; Held shown and Overview first when shown are A797's and the
  master's proposed defaults). `ColumnScope.screen` gains `"all_stations"`, so a till's All stations
  view never shares a stored layout with a pass or monitor the same device later chooses (slice plan
  D16: different screen kinds never share a key). `venueId` is the server's origin and `deviceId` the
  device's id, both handed in by the app as the station's are (`till-app.ts:19213`,
  `#serverOrigin()`); an empty id stores nothing (`kitchen-columns.ts:26-29`). Wide: one column per
  shown key in the chosen order, each collapsible by its heading (count kept). Narrow (the measured
  fit fails, `columnsFit`): one list of sections in the same order, each collapsible by its heading
  (A797 §3.5). Columns opens the existing window (`kitchen-columns-dialog.ts`) from the pass's header
  row. **Revision 6:** built as written in B4a (#2425); in B4b, D95 replaces the `PASS_COLUMNS`
  literal and "Held shown" (the owner's answer to Owner question 6 part 2). The scopes, storage,
  collapse and narrow list stand.

- **D73 What a card and its items say.** Card heading: the kind's word («Nuevo pedido», «Siguiente
  pase», «Añadido»; none on a Held card), the table label or order number(s) with former numbers, the
  group number where there is one, and the age: To fire and In progress in whole minutes since the
  card's `requestedAt` (`station.min`); Ready "Ready {n} min" (`table.row_ready_ago`) since its
  `readyAt`; Held none. **Lateness counts every dish in view, dimmed or not** (revision 2, Owner
  question 2's recommended (b)): the card's accent band is the worst `passItemBand` (D68) of its
  items with `visible: true` and a band (held items have none); a card with none has no band. The
  accent keeps today's pass colours and motion (`till-expo-screen.ts`, `#accentClasses`: the brand
  colour when warm, then red, `flash` on forgotten unless reduced motion; A1250 asks the owner
  whether the pass should use amber like the station, so B4 does not change it). The pass-wide
  overdue count is the number of cards whose band is overdue or worse (today's
  `#overdueOrderCount`, over cards). An item in view whose band is forgotten carries the Forgotten
  mark (`data-forgotten`), dimmed or not. Each item: its name, quantity, extras, note and A669
  allergy line as today's card draws them, its option answers, per-line note, as-served allergen
  chips and diet badges, cross-station extra references (Spanish wording included), identical
  portions drawn as one row (A803, `portionGroups` with `kitchenPortionKey` as today), and one
  caption:

  | Item | Caption | Drawn |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | `phase` `held` | none | as a held dish today (dashed) |
  | `requested`, not informational | "Waiting for the pass" · station | normal |
  | `requested`, informational (another pass's) | "{station}: waiting for its pass" | dimmed |
  | `waiting`/`preparing`/`ready`, not informational | state word · station | normal |
  | `waiting`/`preparing`/`ready`, informational | state word · "Already at {station}" | dimmed |
  | `stationClosed`, `extra: false` | adds "{station} is closed: move the dish …" | the card's Fire is off (B3 D51) |
  | `stationClosed`, `extra: true` | adds "{station} is closed: Fire still sends it there" | Fire stays on |

  Overview on the pass counts the pass's own work only (`informational: false`): `held` and
  `requested` as held (after the +), `waiting` and `preparing` as making, `ready` left out, through
  the one counting function `overviewRows` (A797 §7: no second aggregator). Under D89 a pass with no
  waiting station in view counts every dish in view, as the station's Overview does. Dimmed
  (`informational: true`) is a token style and the caption, never colour alone; the view reads
  `informational` from the board and never recomputes it (D89 decides it on the server).

- **D74 Fire.** A To fire card on a pass (not a monitor, not All stations) shows **Marchar** when the
  device runs the pass (`runsPass`); it is enabled when `releaseTicketItemIds` is not empty, and off
  with the closed-station sentence beside the blocked dish when the board blocked it (B3 D51). A
  press sends exactly `{ submissionId, target, ticketItemIds: releaseTicketItemIds, expectedContext:
  releaseContext }` from the card as last read; nothing is filtered or added (Contract 7). One ask per
  card is in flight at a time; a second press while it is out does nothing. An answer (success or
  refusal) clears the ask and reloads the board. A press that gets no answer (the rejection carries no
  `status`) keeps the ask, with its submission id, marks the board stale (the existing
  `till-stale-since` line), and the next press resends the same ask, which the server replays (B3
  D48); a board read that no longer offers the card, or offers a different context, drops the kept
  ask. `ticket.pass_context_changed` and `party.not_open` reload the board and show the approved
  sentence `station.table_changed` («La pantalla se ha actualizado: revísala y vuelve a pulsar.»),
  true because the reload has happened (B3's "Left for B4"). **Where and how long** (revision 2,
  review-1 I6): in the screen's existing `data-table-changed` status line (`role="status"`), the
  line today's Ready refusal uses, with today's lifecycle for that line: shown by the good read the
  refusal's reload makes, kept through a failed read, and cleared by the next good read after that
  one (the 15-second refresh, `REFRESH_MS`, `till-expo-screen.ts:72`, `#reload` `:627-628`) or by
  the next Fire or group Ready press (`#groupAct`, `:674-675`), whichever comes first; a course
Ready press (`#act`, `:656-666`) does not clear it. Pinned today by
  `till-expo-screen.test.ts` "the next press clears the notice, and another refusal does not raise
  it" and "the next successful read after the one that showed it clears the notice" (`:2410-2425`,
  `:2455-2485`); both map to the new screen unchanged. On a kitchen display, `device.unauthorized`
  and `kitchen_screen.not_allowed` (the release refuses `device.unauthorized` when the display's
  pass screen has gone, `pass-release.ts:50,62`) go through today's levers' path (`#switched(error,
  true)`, `till-expo-screen.ts:686-705`): the screen emits `kitchen-screen-changed` and shows no
  refusal; on a till `kitchen_screen.not_allowed` shows its words and `device.unauthorized` re-reads
  the device's choice (D92). Any other refusal shows that code's existing words in
  the `data-lever-error` line, on a till and on a kitchen display alike (unlike `#leverRefused`,
  `:712-720`, which shows words only on a display or for `allergy.*` codes), which no read clears
  and the next press clears (`#act`, `#groupAct`), which is master T7's "retain action refusals across read
  recovery" (`2026-10-19-a773-2-firing-and-pass.md:311`). Nothing releases automatically after a
  refusal.

- **D75 Ready levers, Reprint and help badges keep today's rules on the new cards.** Group Ready on
  an In progress group card, sending `card.target.partyId` and `card.party.revision` (D69), through
  `bumpDeviceGroupReady` on a kitchen display and `bumpGroupReady` on a till, as today
  (`till-expo-screen.ts:894-926`); a `party.out_of_date` refusal keeps today's "{table} changed."
  line. Course Ready on a bill card, per course whose items on the card are all released and not all
  ready, through `bumpDeviceCourseReady`/`bumpCourseReady` with `target.workingOrderId` and the course
  id (`:1029-1059`). No lever on a Held or To fire card, on a monitor, or for a device without
  `runsPass`. Reprint per bill of a card on a till only (`:842`, `:853-860`). Help badges
  `help.pass.card` (on the first card) and `help.pass.ready` (on a Ready lever) as today. One
  change: on a till a Ready refusal now shows its code's words in `data-lever-error`, as D74 does for
  Fire (today's till shows words only for `allergy.*`), and no read clears them.

- **D76 The old reads retire in B4a, with their probes.** Removed: the routes `GET
  /api/device/pass-screen`, `GET /api/device/pass-monitor`, `GET /api/expo/queue`; `listPassScreen`,
  `listPassMonitor`, `withSections`, `PassCourse`/`PassGroup`/`PassOrder` (`pass-board.ts`);
  `readPassBoard`, `listExpoQueue`, `ExpoCourse`/`ExpoGroup`/`ExpoOrder` (`working-order.ts`), and
  `PassRowRead.servedAt` if only `readPassBoard` read it; the till's `getDevicePassScreen`,
  `getDevicePassMonitor`, `getExpoQueue`, `DevicePassScreen`, `DevicePassMonitor`,
  `ExpoCourse`/`ExpoGroup`/`ExpoOrder` and the `Watcher*` types built on them; `till-expo-screen.ts`
  and its seven test files. `ExpoItem` (server and till) stays. Every server test that used an old
  read as a probe asserts the same fact through `readScopedPassBoard(tx, cfg, EVERY)` and a shared
  helper that flattens a board's items (new `apps/server/src/testing/pass-board-probe.ts`, outside
  coverage like the rest of `src/testing/**`). A probe assertion about a fact the new board does not
  carry is removed only where the fact itself is retired behaviour, and each such removal is listed
  by test name in the PR: per-bill cards and course or group sections on the pass (replaced by B3
  D45's cards), an order-level band (replaced by the card's worst item band), and the old route's
  answer shape. A fact the new board carries is never dropped.

- **D77 `help.pass.card` is rewritten** (it would be false). New words, so Owner question 1; the build
  uses the recommended text meanwhile (owner rule 2026-10-05) and B4a lands only once it is answered.
  The card badge's label reuses `help.kitchen.cards_part` ("Cards" / «Tarjetas», approved, same
  meaning) instead of `help.pass.cards_part` ("Order cards", which names per-order cards); the
  unused key is deleted from both languages (revision 2, review-1 M8). The badge links to
  `kitchen-service.service-lifetime` as today until Task A17 writes `#pass-screen`, adds
  `"kitchen-service.pass-screen"` to `apps/till/src/help-guide.ts` and points the badge there
  (review-1 M7).

- **D78 The station's Held column (B4b).** **Revision 5:** which columns a station has, which it
  shows by default, where a held card is drawn and how a saved layout reads back are D94's, which
  supersedes this decision's first version (`STATION_COLUMNS.order` `["overview", "held", "waiting",
  "preparing", "ready"]`, Held a fifth column, D93). What stands here: a card with no released dish
  (`since === null`) is held, dashed and untimed as today; every other card stays in the column of
  its least advanced released dish (unchanged). A held card's heading says "Waiting for the pass" when every dish
  on it is Requested, else "Held, not released"; a Requested dish on a card that is not itself held
  says "Waiting for the pass" where a held dish says "Held, not released" (`#stateWord`,
  `station-queue.ts:1291-1297` at `455432e262`). (Superseded by D94: "Held is drawn only while it
  holds a card (D93)", and the placement of a Held key in a saved layout, `sanitise`,
  `kitchen-columns.ts:54-84`, which D94 reruns with its own defaults.)
  Overview keeps counting a Requested dish as held (`overviewDishes` reads `firedAt`,
  `kitchen-cards.ts`), as A797 §3.3 puts it ("Both are held from the station's point of view").
  Reachable today: a genuinely held card sits under the Held heading at the bottom of Waiting by
  default, and in a Held column on a device that shows one (D94; owner Q42 as the owner restated it
  on 2026-11-03), which makes `help.kitchen.card` false (D90).

- **D79 "at the pass" (B4b).** The table screen's `#rowState` and the Sent list's `#status` say "at
  the pass" («en el pase») for a released row whose own `requestedAt` is set and `firedAt` null —
  the row's stamps, not its `kitchen`'s, so a dish whose extras wait at the pass says it too;
  the Sent list keeps "Fired" for any other row with `firedAt` null. The till's `CurrentOrderKitchen`
  gains `requestedAt` and `phase`, `CurrentOrderRow` gains `requestedAt` (B3 D55), and
  `StationQueueItem` gains `phase`, `requestedAt`, `readyAt` and `passControlled`, all required, as
  the server sends them. The current-order fields land optional first and become required in the
  task that fixes the last fixture (B1a2, B1a3), so each commit typechecks and no task fixes every
  fixture at once (revision 2, review-1 I3).

- **D80 The closed-station alert for dishes waiting for the pass (B4b).** `rerouteHeldAtRelease`
  splits its stranded rows by their station's setting (`passWaitingStations`, read once) and raises
  `route.released_at_closed_station` for the rest, unchanged, and a new incident code
  `route.waiting_at_closed_station` for rows at a station that waits, with params `{ station,
  dishes, workingOrderId, orderNumber }` (no `orderLabel`: the approved words do not use it, and the
  personal-log guard then needs no new row). Its English and Spanish are the approved sheet's row
  exactly (`~/waitron-campaign-c/a773-2b3-owner-questions.md`, Question 1; approved whole
  2026-11-02 ~09:40):
  - English: "Order {orderNumber}: {dishes} are waiting for the pass at {station}, which is closed
    or disabled, and the prep station rules sent them nowhere else. Before the pass fires them, move
    the dishes to another station from the till (Move to station…), or open {station} for today on
    the Prep stations page. Sides that go with a dish cannot be moved: the pass's Fire sends them to
    {station}. Dishes left at a closed station later do not add to this alert while it is open, so
    there may be more."
  - Spanish: «Pedido {orderNumber}: {dishes} están esperando al pase en {station}, que está cerrada
    o deshabilitada, y las reglas de las estaciones de preparación no los enviaron a ningún otro
    sitio. Antes de que el pase los marche, pasa los platos a otra estación desde el TPV (Cambiar de
    estación…) o abre {station} por hoy en la página de Estaciones de preparación. Las guarniciones
    que acompañan a un plato no se pueden mover: al marcharlas, el pase las envía a {station}. Los
    platos que queden más tarde en una estación cerrada no se añaden a esta alerta mientras esté
    abierta, así que puede haber más.»
  Registered in `errors.ts` beside the old code;
  `scripts/alert-codes.test.ts` already lists `closed-station-alert.ts` (`:28`) and claims areas by
  prefix, so `route.` covers it (review M10). The two `pass-release.test.ts` cases of finding 10
  change.

- **D81 Department transfers name a dish waiting for the pass (B4b).** `outstandingWork`
  (`department-transfer-api.ts:288-308`) gains `requestedAt`; the till's widget
  (`widgets/department-transfers.ts:511-529`) says "Waiting for the pass" for a row with
  `requestedAt` set and `firedAt` null, and keeps today's state word otherwise (a held row's
  "Waiting" is a separate, older inaccuracy: backlog).

- **D82 The display setting's storage (B4b; revision 4 per review I2, I4, I8, M5).**
  `device_profile_kitchen_screens.show_non_pass_stations` `flag`, not null, default true;
  `device_kitchen_screens.show_non_pass_stations` `flag`, nullable (null: the profile's). Both
  kinds' rows carry the column; only `pass` and `pass_monitor` read it or ever store a value other
  than the default. **Types** (`packages/module/src/module.ts:575-629`): a profile's entry becomes
  `ProfileKitchenScreen`, a `KitchenScreenScope` plus `showNonPassStations?: boolean`, and
  `ProfileKitchenScreens` maps kinds to it. The field never goes on `KitchenScreenScope`, which
  `DeviceKitchenScreen` shares (`:575-586`; review I8), and `DeviceKitchenScreen` gains nothing: the
  device's choice travels apart from its screens (D83). `ResolvedKitchenScreen` gains, on every kind,
  `nonPassStationsOverride: boolean | null` (the device row's stored value; null when the device's
  effective choice has no screen of the kind, because it never chose one or a narrowing took the
  kind or all its stations, and on a station screen; a narrowing leaves the row and its value, which
  come back with the kind when the profile gives it back) and `showNonPassStations: boolean`, which
  is `nonPassStationsOverride ?? the profile row's value ?? true`. So a device under a till or
  handheld profile with no row of the kind (such a profile bounds every station, `boundOf`,
  `packages/venue-service/src/kitchen-screens.ts:742-750`) shows other stations' dishes, and a
  station screen reads `true` and `null`. Its twins (`apps/till/src/api/client.ts:1756`,
  `apps/dashboard/src/api/client.ts:947`) follow in B6a2; `packages/venue-service/src/dashboard/routing-client.ts`
  imports the module's type, so it follows in B6a1. **Reads:** a profile's pass and pass-monitor
  entries carry `showNonPassStations`; a station entry carries no such key, so pins of station-only
  profiles do not move (review I8). **Writers:** a profile write that omits the field keeps the
  stored value per kind (read before `replaceProfileKitchenScreens`, `:157-200`, deletes); a new
  kind starts true; copy copies it. `setDeviceKitchenScreens` (`:537-569`) deletes and reinserts a
  device's rows, so it reads each kind's stored value first and writes it back for every kind the
  new pick keeps (a dropped kind loses it; a re-added kind starts null). Narrowing never touches a
  device's rows ("The device's stored choice is never changed", `narrowDevices`, `:753-758`) and
  never reports the value as lost access. New seat `setDeviceKitchenScreenDisplay(tx, deviceId,
  kind: "pass" | "pass_monitor", value: boolean | null): Promise<void>`, no `cfg` (slice plan D17),
  updating only that column of the device's row of that kind. It refuses `device.unauthorized` for
  a `station` kind (or any kind other than `pass` and `pass_monitor`), and unless the device's
  EFFECTIVE screens, after any narrowing, hold a screen of that kind: a kind the device never chose,
  or one a narrowing took (the kind, or all its stations or zones), is refused even though its row
  and stored value remain (B7a case 3). **The bundle:** profile values travel in the configuration
  bundle (the table is listed whole, `packages/venue-service/src/configuration-transfer.ts:708`);
  device rows do not travel (not listed). No format bump (review M5, read): the import refuses a row
  carrying a column its table lacks (`packages/provisioning/src/configuration-transfer.ts:671-676`)
  and inserts the columns a row has, so a bundle exported before B4b imports after it with the
  column at its default true (B5 case 6 runs this half), and one exported after B4b is refused by an
  older box as `setup.request_invalid` (`table:device_profile_kitchen_screens`), not
  `setup.configuration_outdated`. Before go-live that is acceptable (CLAUDE.md §3: no
  backwards-compatibility code until production). No `location_id`, no backfill, no reset.

- **D83 The display setting's routes (B4b; revision 4 per review I3, I4, I5, M2, M7).**
  **Profiles:** `parseProfileKitchenScreens` (`apps/server/src/device.ts:381-400`) accepts
  `showNonPassStations` (boolean) on a `pass` or `pass_monitor` entry and refuses it on a `station`
  entry or with another type: `management.request_invalid` with `field: "kitchenScreens"`, the
  field of the parser's other refusals (`device.ts:349`), writing nothing.
  **A device's choice travels apart from its screens** (review I4). If it rode in the device's
  `kitchenScreens`, the dashboard would send `kitchenScreens` whenever the edit's payload differs
  from what was opened (`#sendScreens`, `apps/dashboard/src/screens/devices-screen.ts:1315-1320`),
  and `setDeviceKitchenScreens` deletes the device's removal records with its rows
  (`packages/venue-service/src/kitchen-screens.ts:545-548`); so flipping only the choice on a
  narrowed device would turn the narrowing into the device's own pick, and a later profile
  widening would not give the stations back. Instead `PATCH /management-api/devices/:id`
  (`apps/server/src/device-api.ts:833`) takes a separate body field `kitchenScreenDisplay: { pass?:
  boolean | null; pass_monitor?: boolean | null }`: a key absent keeps that kind's value; another
  key, or a value of another type, is refused `management.request_invalid` with `field:
  "kitchenScreenDisplay"`. It is parsed beside `kitchenScreens` (`:866`) and written through the
  seat after `setDeviceKitchenScreens` (`:913`) in the same transaction; a key for a kind the device's
  effective screens do not hold after that write (never chosen, or taken by a narrowing) is refused
  the same way (the seat's `device.unauthorized`, D82, answered as that 400), and nothing of the request is written.
  `parseKitchenScreens` (`device.ts:363-377`) is unchanged: it reads only `kind` and the two lists,
  so a stray key in an entry is ignored, as today.
  **Joins cannot set it** (review I5). The accept route reads only `name`, `profileId` and
  `kitchenScreens` from its body (`apps/server/src/join-api.ts:327-334`), so a device a join
  creates has null rows and its profile's value applies; a returning device's rejoin goes through
  `setDeviceKitchenScreens` (`join-requests.ts:536-546`), which carries a stored value forward as
  for every caller (D82). Pinned by B7a case 4; the dashboard's pair dialog draws no such field
  (D84).
  `GET` answers carry the stored values (a profile's entries; a device's through
  `ResolvedKitchenScreen`).
  **The device's own route:** `PUT /api/device/kitchen-screen-display` takes `{ kind,
  showNonPassStations: boolean | null }` under `leverRun`, the release's error boundary
  (`device-api.ts:525`, `:529-553`), with the release's device proof (`requireDeviceProof` before
  the transaction, `assertDeviceStillProven` inside), the effective screen of that kind
  (`kitchenScreenOf`, `:1042-1056`: `device.unauthorized` without it, `session.required` on a
  non-KDS device with nobody signed in), and no cooking action (master Contract 12: presentation
  only). It is not added to `DEVICE_OPEN_WHILE_DISK_FULL` (`:225-231`): on a full disk it is refused
  like every write outside that list, and the pass keeps its last choice.
  **The boards:** the pass board passes `{ showNonPassStations: screen.showNonPassStations }` and
  the monitor `{ release: false, showNonPassStations }` to `readScopedPassBoard`, whose options are
  `Omit<PassBoardOptions, "aStationInViewWaits">` (`apps/server/src/pass-board.ts:55-60`; review
  M2), and both answer `display: { showNonPassStations, nonPassStationsOverride }` beside `board`,
  `stations` and `zones`. The till's All stations view builds its answer by hand
  (`apps/till/src/screens/till-pass-screen.ts:303`) and gives it `display: { showNonPassStations:
  true, nonPassStationsOverride: null }` (D70). The setting never changes a card's column,
  readiness, release ids or context (B3 D46; master Contract 9).
  **A card Hide empties stays** (review I3). Hide moves a card's informational items to its
  `hiddenItems` after the card is placed (`buildPassBoard`, `apps/server/src/pass-card.ts`), so a
  card whose in-view dishes are all informational stays in its column with its heading, age and
  levers and no dish line. **Hidden dishes travel on the card and are counted** (B6b1 review I1,
  2026-11-03): `items` is what the till draws; `hiddenItems` (absent when none) is drawn nowhere and
  left out of the overview facts, but the till's card colour (its band, Forgotten included), the pass-wide
  overdue count, the allergy marker on the card's heading and a bill card's course Ready levers
  (which buttons, and in what order) read `items` and `hiddenItems` together, the till first putting
  them back in the server's order by bill and course place, so Hide changes only the drawn lines
  (D73; hide-fix review I1/M1, 914f3aa5ca). One limit: the server breaks a tie between two courses
  of one bill that share a place by line number (`itemOrder`, `apps/server/src/pass-card.ts:199-208`),
  which a `PassCardItem` does not carry, so with such a tie Hide may list the same course Ready
  buttons in another order (hide-fix-2 review M1, probes P1/P2, control P1c). A tie is reachable only
  through the course API, two managers adding a course at once, or a course made inactive while one
  of its dishes is in the kitchen; the dashboard otherwise numbers courses apart. The hidden dishes ride in a separate list rather than as a flag on
  each dish because B3's case 9 and B4a's case 5 in `pass-card.test.ts` pin `card.items` under Hide
  as the shown dishes only. B6b1 pins it and B6b2 checks the till draws it. Dropping the card would
  take its Ready lever off the screen, which the comparison of the same card on the shown and the
  hidden board (master Review Focus 3) rules out; B12 looks at it.
  **What Hide hides** (revision 2, D89): the board's `informational` dishes. At a pass with no
  waiting station in view and no pass work it sees, that is only dishes outside its view (a station
  or zone it does not cover, on a card it shares); once a station in view waits, also the dishes of
  stations in view that do not wait. So at a default venue's single pass covering every station,
  Hide hides nothing, and the Columns window and the dashboard say nothing more about it (no new
  words); the guide says it (B11).

- **D84 The dashboard's controls (B4b; revision 4 per review I4, I5, M4, M7).** Device profiles:
  under each of the pass and pass monitor choices, a `wt-switch` labelled
  `device_profiles.show_other_stations` "Show other stations' dishes on the pass" / «Mostrar en el
  pase los platos de otras estaciones» (the approved sheet), on by default, part of the profile's
  draft and its Save state, sent inside that kind's `kitchenScreens` entry. Devices: a `wt-combobox`
  with the same label (the same key: one meaning) and three options: `devices.use_profile_setting`
  "Use the profile's setting" / «Usar la configuración del perfil» (the approved sheet; kept over
  «Según el perfil», owner 2026-11-02 ~19:45 item 6) for null, `devices.other_stations_show` "Show"
  / «Mostrar» for true, `devices.other_stations_hide` "Hide" / «Ocultar» for false. Show and Hide are
  the words `table.show_column` and `table.hide_column` use (`apps/dashboard/src/i18n/strings.ts:795-796`,
  `:4556-4557`), under new keys for a new meaning. One combobox under each pass or pass-monitor
  screen in the edit's draft, part of the edit draft (`#editSnapshot`, `#registerEditDraft`), sent
  as `kitchenScreenDisplay` and left out of `#screensPayload`, so a change of the choice alone keeps
  `#sendScreens` false and sends no `kitchenScreens` (D83). The pair dialog draws the same identity
  fields (`#renderIdentityFields`, `devices-screen.ts:2140`, which calls `#renderKitchenFields` at
  `:2188`, for both the pair and the edit dialogs) but not this one, and `#pairPayload` (`:522-528`)
  sends no display value (D83: joins cannot set it). A `management.request_invalid` naming
  `kitchenScreens` or `kitchenScreenDisplay` comes only from a dashboard bug (the controls send
  valid values for kinds the draft holds), so B8 and B9 show it as the form's request refusal line,
  not beside a field (review M7).

- **D85 The pass's Columns window sets the device's choice (B4b; revision 4 per review I6, M4).**
  On a pass and a monitor (not All stations), the window gains a section headed `pass.other_stations`
  "Other stations' dishes" / «Platos de otras estaciones» with three choices: `pass.use_profile`
  "Use the profile's setting" / «Usar la configuración del perfil», `pass.other_stations_show`
  "Show" / «Mostrar» and `pass.other_stations_hide` "Hide" / «Ocultar» (the approved sheet's words;
  Show and Hide as the till already says them, `station.columns_show`, `strings.ts:696`/`:2567`,
  and `find_page.hide`, `:647`/`:2521`, under new keys for a new meaning), drawn with
  `wt-segmented-control` (`packages/ui/src/components/wt-segmented-control.ts`, which has its own
  a11y suite; not yet used in the till, so B10 imports it; review-1 M14). A choice sends `PUT
  /api/device/kitchen-screen-display` and reloads the board; while it is out the choices are
  disabled. **A refusal follows D74 and D92** (review I6): on a kitchen display,
  `device.unauthorized` and `kitchen_screen.not_allowed` close the window and emit
  `kitchen-screen-changed`, showing nothing (the app re-reads the device's screens, as after a
  refused Fire); on a till, `device.unauthorized` closes the window, re-reads the device's choice
  (`#loadChoice`) and shows `station.table_changed` with D74's lifecycle, never the join words
  ("This device isn't set up — ask to join this venue", false here and sending the person to
  somebody else); any other refusal keeps the previous choice and shows that code's existing words
  in the window until the next choice. The window is otherwise unchanged (A797 9b).

- **D86 Owner Q5 (a) needs no change.** Another station's waiting dish already reads `held: true`
  in the rest of the order (`readRestOfOrder`, B2 D33), prints «(en espera)» on a cook's ticket
  (pinned by B2.6a case 6) and shows «En espera» on the station's list (`station-queue.ts:1030` at
  `455432e262`,
  pinned by `station-queue.test.ts:167`).

- **D87 No new words for the release's other refusals.** `management.request_invalid` (only a bug,
  since the till sends the board's own ids), `device.forbidden_action` and
  `service_zone.not_allowed` show their existing words after a Fire, and the last two after a Ready
  on a till (D75). `device.forbidden_action`'s "Ask a manager to change it." sends the person to
  someone else, and `service_zone.not_allowed`'s "Choose one of its own areas" does not fit the pass;
  both strings predate A773-2 and are used elsewhere, so B4 does not change them; they go to the
  backlog, naming Fire and Ready (Task A19 checks their origin with `git log -S` before writing that).

- **D88 The unpaid-departure refusal names a dish waiting for the pass (B4b).** B2's D41 (a dish
  waiting for the pass and not fully served stops "Record unpaid departure") has had today's sentence
  since B2. The approved sheet's row replaces `unpaid_departure.unfired_dishes` in
  `apps/till/src/i18n/codes.ts:158-161` with: "A bill on this table holds items the kitchen is not
  making: not sent, on hold, recalled, or waiting for the pass and not fully served. Cancel them first,
  then record the departure again" / «Una cuenta de esta mesa tiene artículos que cocina no está
  preparando: sin enviar, en espera, retirados, o esperando al pase y sin servir del todo. Cancélalos
  primero y vuelve a registrar la salida». Built in Task B2.

- **D89 Dimming means something only once the pass controls something** (revision 2, review-1 C1;
  built on Owner question 2's recommended (b), which the owner chose 2026-11-02 ~19:45).
  A797 §3.5 dims "Dishes from stations that do not wait for the pass" and Q51 (d) approved showing
  them dimmed (OWNER ANSWER 2026-10-24 04:29, `~/waitron-campaign-c/questions.md`: "every
  recommendation in (a)–(g) stands as written"). Neither looked at a board where no station waits,
  which is every venue until PR C (finding 14). The rule, on the server: a board **dims** when a
  station in the pass's view waits for the pass now (any `kitchen_stations.waits_for_pass` among
  `scope.stationIds`, every station when null; active stations only, `kitchen_stations.active`,
because a deactivated station left waiting would otherwise dim the pass with nothing on any screen
saying why; `passWaitingStations` counts inactive ones, "inactive ones included",
`kitchen-release.ts:5`), or when it holds a dish in view that is pass work
  (`passControlled`, so a To fire card asked for before its station stopped waiting never sits on a
  board that dims nothing). `cardItem`'s `informational` becomes `!visible || (dims && !passWork)`.
  So with no station in view waiting, nothing in view is dimmed, Overview counts every dish in view
  and Hide (B4b) hides nothing in view; as soon as one waits, its dishes that wait for this pass show in full and the
  others in view dim (a dish sent to that station before it began waiting is not `passControlled`
  and stays dimmed, B3 D46). A dish outside the pass's view (on a card it shares with another pass's
  stations or zones) stays informational in every case, as B3 D46 has it: it is never this pass's
  to act on. Lateness ignores `informational` altogether (D73: every dish in view).
  **Where it lives: the server** (`readScopedPassBoard` reads the flag, `buildPassBoard` takes it as
  a fourth argument, default false, and also derives the pass-work half from its rows; as built in
  B4a it is the option `PassBoardOptions.aStationInViewWaits`, `pass-card.ts:289-305`), because
  three readers act on `informational`: the till's dimming, the board's own Overview facts
  (`pass-card.ts:339-353` at `455432e262`) and B4b's Hide, which moves informational items to the
  card's `hiddenItems` in `buildPassBoard` (D83). Deciding it in the till would leave Hide emptying every card at a default venue and the
  overview facts disagreeing with the screen. Release ids and context never read it (B3 D47), so
  Fire is unchanged (A2b's control). Changed check: `pass-board.requested.test.ts` "shows a held
  group as a held card with nothing to fire" (`:477-481`), probe P4's only red.

- **D90 `help.kitchen.card` is rewritten (B4b; Owner question 3, answered as recommended
  2026-11-02 ~19:45 item 3).** B4b's Held column makes two of its sentences false (finding 12).
  Built with the approved text in Task B1b2, English and Spanish (Owner question 3's table).
  **Revision 5:** under D94 a held card sits at the bottom of Waiting by default, so the approved
  text's "is in the Held column" is false by default. Task B1d4 builds the replacement in Owner
  question 6's recommended text; the rest of Owner question 3's text stands. **Revision 6:** the
  owner approved that text and the Columns badge's sentence as proposed (2026-11-03 ~14:10 and
  ~16:10); B1d4 built both in `b0e96167a9`, read at `apps/till/src/i18n/help-strings.ts:49-53` and
  `:110-114` against the answer's words, character for character. D95 needs no help text change
  (D95 "Words").

- **D91 `help.more.pass` is rewritten (B4a; Owner question 4, answered as recommended ~19:45).** Its
  "open orders, each with its dishes grouped by course" is false once the pass shows group cards in
  columns (finding 12). Built with the recommended text meanwhile, in Task A9.

- **D92 On a till, Fire refused `device.unauthorized` re-reads the device's choice like a display,
  never shows the join words (refusal rule)** (driver, 2026-11-02, A8 review Important 1). The
  release refuses `device.unauthorized` when the device's pass screen has gone
  (`pass-release.ts:61-62`), where "This device isn't set up — ask to join this venue" is false and
  sends the person to somebody else. The till runs its choice read (`#loadChoice`), shows what the
  choice now gives (All stations, the monitor, or the screen's "no longer available" line) and, on a
  board, the sentence `station.table_changed` with D74's lifecycle. `kitchen_screen.not_allowed` on
  a till keeps its words (no release throws it today).

- **D93 (superseded by D94, revision 5: the owner answered Owner question 5 on 2026-11-03 ~12:33
  with a shape none of (a)–(c) was; kept as the record of what B1b1 built in `7cb91f7ec4` and
  `52ea90099d`) The station draws Held only while it holds a card** (B4b; revision 4, review C1; built on
  Owner question 5's recommended (b), asked 2026-11-03 ~09:55 and pending, as D89 was built on
  Owner question 2's before it was answered; blocks only B1b1's final shape and B4b's landing).
  **Why:** the station switches from columns to one-column tabs when its shown columns do not fit
  (`columnsFit`, `apps/till/src/state/kitchen-board-fit.ts:19-32`). Four columns need 905 px of
  board at 14 px text and 1103 px at 18 px; five need 1148 px and about 1412 px
  (`kitchen-board-fit.test.ts:16-40`, the "Held as a fifth column" case for 1148). A 1024 px
  tablet's station board is 944 px wide (`station-queue.test.ts:3100`), so a Held column drawn
  always would turn every such station into tabs the day B4b lands, whatever it holds; collapsing
  Held to a strip does not save it (905 + 44 + 12 = 961 px). Review probe R4 (Held drawn always)
  turned 21 cases in 4 files red, among them that 1024 px case.
  **The rule:** the board's shown columns (`#shown`, `widgets/station-queue.ts:749-754`) include
  `held` only when it is not hidden and at least one card has `since === null`; the fit, the tabs
  and the column loop all read that one list, so a station with nothing held draws and measures
  today's columns exactly. Held stays registered (`STATION_COLUMNS`, `stationColumnNames`), so the
  Columns window lists it and a device can move, hide or collapse it; a hidden Held draws none of
  its cards, as any hidden column today. While a held card is on the board, a board narrower than
  its shown columns need shows tabs, Held's included; when the last held card goes, Held goes and
  the columns come back (`willUpdate` already moves a chosen tab that is no longer shown to the
  first, `station-queue.ts:736-739`). The pass is unchanged: its Held is always drawn (D72).
  **If the owner picks (a)** (accept tabs): Held is drawn whenever it is not hidden. B1b1 drops
  case 4's "nothing held" half, case 5's "after release" half and the first mutation control; the
  `:3080` case keeps measuring four columns by hiding Held in its layout, and a new case pins tabs
  at 1024 px with nothing held; "leaves out the columns it cannot draw yet" (`:3842`) changes to
  expect `["overview", "held", "queued", "preparing", "ready"]` with a `toFire` key added; the Goal,
  "The cut" and B13's PR body say every station board under 1148 px at 14 px text shows tabs.
  **If the owner picks (c)** (Held starts hidden): `STATION_COLUMNS.hidden` gains `held`, and a
  held card sits in Held only while Held is shown, else in Waiting, dashed, as today. The
  station-queue rows of "Changed test checks" that move a held card out of Waiting are then not
  changed (their fixtures use the default layout, with Held hidden), while the rows that pin the
  default order and the names list still change; `sanitise` gives a saved layout Held hidden (`kitchen-columns.ts:72-81` copies the default's
  hidden), so case 2 expects Held hidden; case 4 becomes "Held hidden: held cards in Waiting;
  Held shown: in Held"; D78's "a held card moves from Waiting to Held on every station" holds only
  on a device that shows Held; and Owner question 3's approved help text ("is in the Held column")
  is false by default, so it needs new words, a new owner question, before B4b lands.

- **D94 Every station column is a choice, and held cards sit under Waiting by default** (B4b;
  revision 5; supersedes D93 and D78's column sentences). The owner's answer to Owner question 5
  (2026-11-03 ~12:33, relayed by the watcher): "i don't think we should use tabs. i like the option
  of having the held column below waiting. but waiting and overview (in fact all columns) are
  optional, so the user can choose what they want. so i think all columns should be togglable, and
  one special to combine waiting and held". Asked what "no tabs" means, the owner chose: Held never
  forces tabs; today's tab fallback stays only for a screen too narrow for the columns switched on;
  by default Held sits combined under Waiting.
  **The keys.** `ColumnKey` (`state/kitchen-columns.ts:1-2`) gains `"waitingOnly"`, and
  `STATION_COLUMNS` (`:17-21`) becomes `{ order: ["overview", "waiting", "waitingOnly", "held",
  "preparing", "ready"], hidden: ["waitingOnly", "held"], collapsed: [] }`. `sanitise` registers
  only the defaults' keys, so no pass layout ever holds `waitingOnly` (and, under D95, no station
  layout `toFireOnly`). (Revision 6: `PASS_COLUMNS` changes under D95.)
  - **`waiting` is the combined column, "Waiting + Held".** Why the existing key: on `main` the
    station's `waiting` column already holds held cards (`DRAWN.waiting: "queued"` at
    `455432e262`, and `kitchenCards` gives a card with no released dish `column: "queued"`,
    `state/kitchen-cards.ts:103-148`), so every layout a device saved before this branch already
    means "waiting and held cards together" by that key. Keeping it, a saved layout reads back as
    today's four columns with no migration code and no `VERSION` bump (root `CLAUDE.md`: no
    data-migration code before production), and main's checks that read held cards from
    `[data-column="queued"]` stay as they are. It holds every card whose `column` is `"queued"`
    (main's rule): first the cards with a released dish, then, only while one exists, a part
    headed **Held** holding the held cards (`since === null`). Held cards already sort after every
    timed card (an untimed card's age is `Infinity` in `kitchenCards`' sort,
    `kitchen-cards.ts:144-147`), so the part needs no sort of its own and the cards' DOM order is
    main's. Section `data-column="queued"`, tab `data-column-tab="waiting"`, heading toggle
    `data-column-toggle="waiting"`, all as on `main`. Its board heading and tab read
    `station.state.queued` "Waiting" / «Esperando» with the count of all its cards, as today
    (`docs/developers/design-system.md:5857`: "its count (of cards, held cards included…)"). The held
    part is an `h3` with `data-held-part`: `pass.column.held` "Held" / «En espera», its count
    (`data-held-count`), then the held cards, in the column and in the tab panel alike; a collapsed
    column draws neither. The Columns window names the column with a new key
    `station.columns_waiting_held`: "Waiting + Held" / «Esperando + En espera» (Owner question 6
