    carries the words), since the window has to tell it from the separate Waiting.
  - **`waitingOnly` is the separate Waiting:** a card with a released dish whose least advanced dish
    is queued (`column === "queued" && since !== null`, B1b1's `waiting` rule).
    `data-column="waiting-only"`, tab and toggle key `waitingOnly`, heading, tab and window name
    `station.state.queued`.
  - **`held` is the separate Held:** `since === null`, `data-column="held"`, named
    `pass.column.held` (B1b1's). **Drawn whenever it is shown, even empty**: D93's "only while it
    holds a card" retires, because the owner's point 4 is that tabs come only from the columns
    switched on, so a shown Held counts in the fit whether or not it holds anything.
  **Overlaps** (Owner question 6; built on its recommended (a), which the owner chose on 2026-11-03
  ~14:10, and Case 2's two Waiting headings "fine as is", ~16:10). The plan reads the owner's
  "one special to combine waiting and held" as a column with its own Show switch; read as a switch,
  it is Owner question 6's (c), under which no overlap can happen. Under (a) each column draws what
  its name says, whatever else is shown; with Waiting + Held and Waiting both shown, two columns
  are headed `station.state.queued` and only the Columns window tells them apart (revision 5 review
  I2). With Waiting + Held and Held both shown, a held card is drawn
  in both; with Waiting + Held and Waiting both shown, a card with a released dish is drawn in both;
  with neither Waiting + Held nor Held shown, held cards are drawn in no column, as a hidden
  column's cards are today, while Overview counts them after its "+" and the header row counts them.
  Nothing switches automatically.
  **Every column may be switched off, as today; no new rule.** `setColumnVisible`
  (`kitchen-columns.ts:140-149`) and the window's Show switch (`kitchen-columns-dialog.ts:157-163`)
  refuse nothing; `main` pins "every column hidden draws no column and keeps Columns"
  (`station-queue.test.ts:4604` at `455432e262`, from `7aaf91841b`) and "with every column hidden
  there are no tabs" (`:4402`, from `1cd0275945`); the guide says so
  (`apps/server/src/guide/en/kitchen-service.md:162-163`, from `7aaf91841b`). Not an open choice.
  **Saved layouts** go through `sanitise` (`kitchen-columns.ts:54-84`) unchanged: a key the stored
  order lacks goes right after the nearest earlier registered key it holds, with the defaults'
  hidden and collapsed for it (`:73-82`). **Run, not read:** the real `readColumnState` with the
  D94 defaults passed as its `defaults` argument (node 26.13.0 `--experimental-strip-types`, a
  script in `/tmp/d94probe/`, no repository file touched, at `934a3885cc`) printed:
  - main's four keys, nothing hidden → `order ["overview","waiting","waitingOnly","held","preparing","ready"]`,
    `hidden ["waitingOnly","held"]`: today's four columns (the failing outcome would have listed
    either new key as shown);
  - main's four with `waiting` hidden and collapsed → `hidden ["waiting","waitingOnly","held"]`,
    `collapsed ["waiting"]`: the device's choice kept;
  - Overview last → `["waiting","waitingOnly","held","preparing","ready","overview"]`, the two new
    keys hidden;
  - saved before Overview, `["ready","waiting","preparing"]` →
    `["overview","ready","waiting","waitingOnly","held","preparing"]`, the two new keys hidden;
  - B1b1's five keys, on devices that ran this unmerged branch only,
    `["overview","held","waiting","preparing","ready"]` →
    `["overview","held","waiting","waitingOnly","preparing","ready"]`, `hidden ["waitingOnly"]`:
    Held stays shown beside Waiting + Held, so held cards are drawn twice until the device switches
    one off (Owner question 6 (a)); no migration, as above;
  - nothing stored → the defaults; Ready moved up from the defaults →
    `["overview","waiting","waitingOnly","held","ready","preparing"]`.
  An arrow beside a hidden row swaps with it and changes nothing on the board; that is today's
  window (the pass's hidden Overview behaves the same way).
  **The fit.** `#shown` returns to `main`'s form (every drawable key not hidden, in the layout's
  order) and `columnsFit` (`state/kitchen-board-fit.ts:19-32`) counts each shown key once, so
  Waiting + Held is one card column and its held part adds nothing. The default layout shows
  Overview, Waiting + Held, Preparing and Ready, today's four: 905 px of board at 14 px text (D93's
  figures, `kitchen-board-fit.test.ts`), which the 1024 px tablet's 944 px board holds, held card
  or not. Separate Waiting and Held instead of the combined column are five columns, 1148 px: a
  1280 px screen at normal text holds them, a 1024 px tablet does not, and either way whether or not
  anything is held. `main`'s 1024 px case ("a long dish name at a 1024 px kitchen display",
  `station-queue.test.ts:3080` at `455432e262`, `:3222` now) keeps pinning `.kanban` on the 944 px
  board with nothing held, under the D94 default (its fixture's `hidden` gains the defaults'); B1d2
  case 2 pins the same board with a held card.
  **Where the choice is stored: per device, in the browser, as every column choice today.**
  `KitchenColumnLayouts` (`state/kitchen-column-layouts.ts:12-67`, B1c) under
  `waitron.kitchenColumns.v1.` keyed by venue, device, screen and station (`kitchen-columns.ts:31-35`),
  and the window says "Chosen on this device" (`station.columns_kept`, `i18n/strings.ts:698`). D82
  stores something else, on the server: the pass's other-stations choice per profile and per
  device. So no server column, route or migration is added for the station's columns.
  **Earlier owner decisions checked.** Q42 ("Station Coming up becomes Held"): held cards still say
  Held, by default under a Held heading in Waiting and as a column on choice; the owner's answer of
  2026-11-03 sets that default. D71/D76 (the pass screen, the retired reads): untouched. D82:
  unchanged (above). Owner question 3's help text: "is in the Held column" becomes false by default
  (D90); Owner question 6 carried the new words, which the owner approved.
  **The pass (superseded by D95, revision 6: the owner chose Owner question 6 part 2's (b) on
  2026-11-03 ~16:05; kept as the record of why the question was asked)** keeps B4a's shape: Held, To fire, In progress and Ready, always drawn unless hidden.
  At 1024 px its board (944 px) is narrower than those four columns need (960 px), so it shows its
  narrow list, held course or not (revision 5 review I2, probe P6; landed in #2425). No cheap change
  keeps both Held and four columns at 944 px: narrowing every card column changes the station's
  fit too, and starting Held hidden takes held cards off a default pass. So B4b leaves the pass as it
  landed and asks (Owner question 6, part 2; recommended: keep it, a follow-up if the owner wants
  the station's rule there).

- **D95 The pass takes the station's shape: held cards at the bottom of To fire by default, and
  Held and To fire apart a choice** (B4b; revision 6; supersedes D72's `PASS_COLUMNS` literal and
  "Held shown", and D94's note on the pass). The owner's answer to Owner question 6 part 2, as the
  watcher relayed it ("WATCHER 2026-11-03 ~16:05" in `~/waitron-campaign-c/questions.md`): "The
  owner chose (b): in B4b, give the pass the station's shape — a combined **Held + To fire** column
  by default (held cards at its bottom under a Held heading, as the station's Waiting + Held), the
  separate **Held** column a choice in Columns. Plan the new pass tasks before B4b lands; follow
  Part 1's (a) rule for overlapping columns on the pass too (each column shows what its name says,
  nothing switches by itself)." The goal, from the question's worked example: a 1024 px pass tablet
  (board 944 px) draws columns by default, held course or not, and turns to its one list only when
  the columns switched on do not fit. The relay names only a separate Held; a separate To fire is
  this plan's reading of "the station's shape", whose combined column also has its separate twin
  (D94's `waitingOnly`).
  **The keys.** `ColumnKey` (`state/kitchen-columns.ts:1-2`) gains `"toFireOnly"`, and
  `PASS_COLUMNS` (`:23-27`) becomes `{ order: ["overview", "toFire", "held", "toFireOnly",
  "inProgress", "ready"], hidden: ["overview", "held", "toFireOnly"], collapsed: [] }`, for the
  pass, the pass monitor and a till's All stations alike (D72 gives the three one default; each
  keeps its own stored layout).
  - **`toFire` is the combined column, "Held + To fire".** Why the existing key: it is the column
    the owner's answer extends ("held cards at its bottom"), headed **To fire** on the board as the
    station's combined column keeps Waiting's heading, so a layout that moved, hid or collapsed To
    fire keeps meaning the same column. Unlike the station's `waiting` (D94), `main`'s `toFire`
    (`a5422c5f6c`, #2425) never held held cards, so for the pass "Saved layouts" below is not "no
    change". It draws the server's To fire cards in the server's order, then, only while one exists,
    the held part, then the server's held cards in the server's order. Section
    `data-column="toFire"`, toggle `data-column-toggle="toFire"`, as on `main`. Heading
    `pass.column.to_fire` "To fire" / «Por marchar» with the count of all its cards, held cards
    included, as the station's Waiting counts its held cards (`docs/developers/design-system.md:5857`).
    The held part is the station's, drawn by one helper both boards share (B1e1): an `h3` with
    `data-held-part`, `pass.column.held` "Held" / «En espera», an `aria-hidden` " ·" and
    `[data-held-count]`; a collapsed column draws neither its cards nor the part. In the narrow list
    the section draws the same. The Columns window names it with a new key
    `pass.columns_held_to_fire`: "Held + To fire" / «En espera + Por marchar» — the owner's own
    naming, in the owner's order, built from the two column words the owner already approved
    (`i18n/strings.ts:768-769` and `:2634-2635`), as the station's «Esperando + En espera» was.
  - **`held` is the separate Held:** B4a's column unchanged (`data-column="held"`,
    `pass.column.held`, the server's held cards), hidden by default. The same key as the station's
    separate Held (D94).
  - **`toFireOnly` is the separate To fire:** the server's To fire cards only,
    `data-column="toFireOnly"` (the pass names each section by its key, `#column` in
    `widgets/pass-board.ts`), heading and window name `pass.column.to_fire`, hidden by default. The
    station's `waitingOnly` pattern.
  - **In progress, Ready and Overview** are unchanged; Overview stays hidden at first (the owner's,
    D72).
  **Cards.** `passCardViews` (`state/pass-view.ts`) still builds one view per card from the server's
  four columns; a board column draws views and never builds them. So a card drawn in two columns is
  one view: the overdue count (`passOverdueCount`) counts it once, and a held card keeps its view's
  `column: "held"` wherever it is drawn (dashed, untimed and without buttons, `cardView`), and a To
  fire card its Fire (`fire.shown` reads the card's server column), which `sending` disables in every
  copy.
  **Overlaps follow the owner's rule (a) on the pass too:** each column draws what its name says,
  whatever else is shown, and nothing switches by itself. Held + To fire with Held: a held card is
  drawn in both. Held + To fire with To fire: a To fire card is drawn in both, with two Fire buttons
  that send the same card, and both columns are headed To fire (the station's Case 2, which the
  owner left "fine as is"; only the Columns window tells them apart). Neither Held + To fire nor
  Held: held cards are on no column; Overview, when shown, still counts their dishes after its "+"
  (`passOverviewDishes`), as a hidden column's cards are counted today.
  **The fit.** `columnsFit` (`state/kitchen-board-fit.ts:19-32`) is unchanged and counts each shown
  key once, so Held + To fire is one card column and its held part adds nothing. **Run, not read**
  (node 26.13.0 `--experimental-strip-types`, the real `columnsFit` on copies of
  `kitchen-board-fit.ts` and `kitchen-columns.ts` in `/tmp/d95probe/`, no repository file touched,
  at `5be5f41e83`; the sizes B4a's fit uses: 16.5 × text px per card column, 11 rem Overview,
  12 px gaps, a 44 px strip): the D95 default's three card columns need 717 px, so a 944 px board
  draws columns at 14 px text and at 18 px (915 px); the failing case, B4a's default of four card
  columns, needs 960 px and printed the list at 944, as the revision 5 review's P6 measured on the
  screen. Overview shown as well: 905 px, columns at 944 at 14 px text, the list at 18 px. Held and
  To fire apart (four card columns): 960 px, the list at 944, columns on a 1264 px board. All six
  shown: 1391 px, the list even on a 1264 px board. What 130% root text gives on a real 1024 px
  screen is B12's to record (the probe also printed columns with every size scaled by 1.3: 932 px).
  **Saved layouts** go through `sanitise` unchanged. **Run, not read** (the same probe, the real
  `readColumnState` with D95's defaults passed in):
  - nothing stored → D95's defaults, shown `["toFire","inProgress","ready"]` (the failing outcome
    would have shown `held`);
  - B4a's default saved after a collapse (`["overview","held","toFire","inProgress","ready"]`,
    hidden `["overview"]`, collapsed `["inProgress"]`) → order
    `["overview","held","toFireOnly","toFire","inProgress","ready"]`, hidden
    `["overview","toFireOnly"]`, collapsed kept: Held stays shown beside Held + To fire, so held cards
    are drawn twice (rule (a)) and the four card columns keep that device on the list at 1024 px, as
    before, until someone switches Held off;
  - B4a's with Held hidden → the same order, hidden `["overview","held","toFireOnly"]`: exactly the
    new default's columns;
  - B4a's with Overview shown → Overview, Held, Held + To fire, In progress and Ready shown.
  A pass stores a layout only once someone moves, shows, hides or collapses a column on it
  (`KitchenColumnLayouts.keep`, `state/kitchen-column-layouts.ts`), and #2425 landed on 2026-11-03
  at 08:28, so only a device someone did that on since then reads the second or fourth line. No
  migration code (root `CLAUDE.md`: none before production; any installation may be reset) and no
  `VERSION` bump (`kitchen-columns.ts:29`: one version for every screen, so a bump would also throw
  away every station's layout). **Considered and not chosen:** giving the separate Held a new key
  (say `heldOnly`), so every saved pass layout would drop `held` and read back as the new shape. It
  would name the same separate Held column differently on the station and the pass, to spare a few
  hours of saved layouts on a product with no live venue; this plan keeps one key per meaning, as
  D94 did for B1b1's five-key layouts.
  **Words.** Nothing new on the board: "To fire" and "Held" are already there. One new name in the
  Columns window (above). The help texts need no change, read at `apps/till/src/i18n/help-strings.ts`:
  `help.pass.card` (`:58-59`, `:119-120`) never says where a held card sits; `help.kitchen.columns`
  (`:52-53`, `:113-114`), which the pass's Columns badge shows (`widgets/pass-board.ts`, `render`),
  is already the owner's approved sentence ("shows or hides each one; some start hidden"), true of
  the pass now that Held and To fire apart start hidden; `help.more.pass` (`:45-46`, `:106-107`)
  says the pass shows cards "in columns from held to ready", still true (held cards come first, at
  the bottom of the first column, and Ready last). The guide is not: `#pass-screen` says "Its
  columns are **Held**, **To fire**, **In progress** and **Ready**" (`kitchen-service.md` en
  `:171-177`, es `:196-203`), and `#request-cooking` says a held card "waits in **Held**" (en `:273`,
  es `:305`); B11 rewrites both.
  **Where the choice is stored:** per device, in the browser, as D94 says; no server change.
  **Earlier decisions checked.** D72: its scopes, storage, collapse and narrow list stand; "Held
  shown" goes, by the owner's answer (Departures). D71, D73–D76, D89: unchanged (cards, Fire, levers,
  dimming). D85 (Task B10): the window's other-stations section is independent of its rows; B10 is
  unchanged. A797 §3.5 and Q42 ("held dishes get their own column"): held cards still say Held, under
  a Held heading by default and in a column on choice, as on the station.

## Guard suites

Each run separately; read exit status and a nonzero `Tests` count.

- **Fiscal pair, unedited** (A2, A2b, A19, B5, B13):
  `pnpm --filter @waitron/fiscal-verifactu exec vitest run src/write-path.e2e.test.ts src/inmutabilidad.test.ts`.
- **Schema (B5, B13):** `pnpm exec vitest run scripts/schema-constraints.test.ts scripts/append-only-triggers.test.ts scripts/behavioural-triggers.test.ts scripts/migrations-match-schema.test.ts scripts/classification-complete.test.ts scripts/append-only-migration-sets.test.ts scripts/module-graph-honesty.test.ts scripts/id-columns-are-references.test.ts scripts/two-file-foreign-keys.test.ts scripts/journal-monotonic.test.ts scripts/migration-upgrade.test.ts scripts/apply-migrations-callers.test.ts`,
  then `pnpm --filter @waitron/composition exec vitest run src/composition.test.ts`. B4a has no
  schema change; if any B4a task touches a schema, stop.
- **Profile maps (A3, A13c, A14b, A19, B7a, B7b, B13):** `pnpm --filter @waitron/server exec vitest run src/till-api.profile-actions.test.ts src/till-api.profile-zones.test.ts`
  (weaker than their names: the maps are comments; the cases are the check).
- **Codes and alerts (A19, B4, B13):** `pnpm exec vitest run scripts/errors-reachable.test.ts scripts/alert-codes.test.ts scripts/personal-log-params.test.ts`.
- **Guides (A17, B11; and A9, which edits help texts):** `pnpm --filter @waitron/server exec vitest run src/guide-content.test.ts src/guide-pages.test.ts src/guide-catalogue.test.ts`, `pnpm exec vitest run scripts/guide-links.test.ts`.
- **UI and code guards (A19, B13):** `pnpm exec vitest run scripts/english-only.test.ts scripts/module-seams.test.ts scripts/native-form-fields.test.ts scripts/style-token-names.test.ts scripts/primary-coloured-text.test.ts scripts/write-path-tables.test.ts`.

## Owner questions

**Question 1 (asked ~18:50; answered 2026-11-02 ~19:45 item 2, with the ~20:00 clause accepted
~19:55; built in B4a): the pass card's help text.**
Today's `help.pass.card` says "One card per order, its dishes by course, or by group on a table's
bill. The minutes count from when the order was opened. …", which the new board makes false.
Recommended, to approve as written or change by quoting. **Revision 2 changes the recommendation
asked at ~18:50** (the watcher relays the new text): the last sentence follows D89 (dimming only
once a station the pass covers waits) and the Forgotten clause comes back, because the new card
keeps the mark (D73). Revision 3 (round-3 Minor 7) adds the dishes sent before their station began
waiting to the last sentence.

| | Text |
| --- | --- |
| English | One card for each group a table sends, across its bills, or for each counter order. Its minutes count from when the waiter sent it, or on a Ready card from when its last dish was ready. The left edge takes the brand colour, then turns red, as its dishes wait, by their stations' late flags; a dish that waits too long says Forgotten. Dimmed dishes are shown for information only: dishes this pass does not cover and, once a station it covers waits for the pass, dishes of the stations that do not and dishes sent before their station began waiting. |
| Spanish | Una tarjeta por cada grupo que envía una mesa, en todas sus cuentas, o por cada pedido de mostrador. Sus minutos cuentan desde que el camarero lo envió o, en una tarjeta lista, desde que su último plato estuvo listo. El borde izquierdo toma el color de marca y luego pasa a rojo mientras sus platos esperan, según los avisos de demora de sus estaciones; un plato que espera demasiado dice Olvidado. Los platos atenuados se muestran solo como información: los que este pase no cubre y, cuando una estación que cubre espera al pase, los de las estaciones que no esperan y los enviados antes de que su estación empezara a esperar. |

Worked example: Grill waits for the pass and the Bar does not. Table 7's burger (Grill) and beer
(Bar) are one group. The pass shows one card under **En curso**, "Mesa 7 · 3 min"; the beer's line
is dimmed and reads "Listo · Ya en Barra". At a venue where nothing waits, neither line is dimmed.
With help on, the badge on the card reads the text above. The alternative is to drop the card's
help badge until a later help pass (backlog), which needs no words now. The badge's label becomes
"Cards" / «Tarjetas», the station's approved label (D77), instead of "Order cards".

**Question 2 (asked ~19:15; answered (b) 2026-11-02 ~19:45 item 1, "WATCHER 2026-11-02 ~19:45" in
`~/waitron-campaign-c/questions.md`; built in B4a as D89): when no station waits for the pass,
should the pass dim every dish?** As asked in `~/waitron-campaign-c/questions.md`
("LANE C 2026-11-02 ~19:15"). The rule approved in A797 Q51 (d) (A797 design §3.5: "Dishes from
stations that do not wait for the pass … are listed in their group dimmed, with their state and
station … for information only"; OWNER ANSWER 2026-10-24 04:29: "every recommendation in (a)–(g)
stands as written") dims every dish at a venue where no station waits, which is every venue until
PR C (finding 14). Worked example: Saturday 21:00, nothing set to wait; Table 4's burger has been at
the Grill for 25 minutes (late).
- (a) Every dish is still dimmed, but lateness counts every dish: Table 4's card turns red.
- **(b) Recommended, and built meanwhile (D89):** dimming only means something when some station in
  the pass's view waits. When none does, nothing in its view is dimmed: the pass looks like today's
  with the new columns, and Table 4's card turns red. As soon as one waits, its dishes that wait for this pass show in
  full and the others dim. Lateness counts every dish in view, dimmed or not. A dish from a station or
  zone this pass does not cover, on a card it shares with another pass, stays dimmed in every case
  (it is never this pass's to act on); at a single pass covering every station there are none.
- (c) As first planned: everything dimmed, no lateness colour, until a station waits.
Under every choice, a held course's card no longer turns red: a held dish has no age (D68, D73).
If the owner chooses (a) or (c), A2b's rule and its committed case change, and Question 1's last
sentence with them.

**Question 3 (answered as recommended 2026-11-02 ~19:45 item 3, "Spanish as in the plan"; built in
B1b2): the station card's help text.** B4b's Held
column (D78) makes two sentences of `help.kitchen.card` false: "in the column of its least advanced
dish" (a card with no dish sent to be cooked is now in Held) and "A card with a dashed border is
held until a waiter requests cooking" (after PR C a dashed card in Held may already have been
requested, and is waiting for the pass). Recommended, built meanwhile (D90), to approve or change by
quoting; the last two sentences are today's, unchanged:

| | Text |
| --- | --- |
| English | Each card is a group a table sent, or a counter order. A card none of whose dishes has been sent to this station to be cooked is in the Held column, with a dashed border and no minutes: it says Held, not released until a waiter requests cooking, and Waiting for the pass once the waiter has asked and the pass has not yet fired it. Every other card is in the column of its least advanced dish; its minutes count from when its first dish was sent to this station to be cooked, or moved here. Tap a dish to move it on one step (on a Whole ticket card, with every dish at its step), or the card's heading to move on the dishes holding it back. Its top edge turns from green to amber, then its whole border red, as the card waits, by the station's late flags. |
| Spanish | Cada tarjeta es un grupo que envió una mesa, o un pedido de mostrador. Una tarjeta sin ningún plato mandado a preparar a esta estación está en la columna En espera, con el borde discontinuo y sin minutos: dice En espera, sin marchar hasta que un camarero pida la preparación, y Esperando al pase cuando ya la ha pedido y el pase aún no la ha marchado. Las demás tarjetas están en la columna de su plato menos avanzado; sus minutos cuentan desde que su primer plato se mandó a preparar a esta estación, o se movió aquí. Toca un plato para avanzarlo un paso (en una tarjeta que dice Toda la comanda, con todos los platos de su paso), o la cabecera de la tarjeta para avanzar los platos que la retienen. Su borde superior pasa de verde a ámbar, y después todo el borde a rojo, mientras la tarjeta espera, según los avisos de demora de la estación. |

Worked example: a table's mains are held. Before B4b the Grill sees them dashed under **Esperando**;
after B4b, dashed under **En espera**, saying «En espera, sin marchar». Once PR C lets the Grill wait
and the waiter asks for the mains, the same card says «Esperando al pase» until the pass presses
Marchar, when it moves to **Esperando** with its minutes.

**Question 4 (answered as recommended 2026-11-02 ~19:45 items 4 and 5: the text below, and «Curso
listo» kept; built in B4a): the More menu's Pass help, and one word for FYI.** `help.more.pass` says the Pass "Opens the pass: the open orders, each with its
dishes grouped by course, or by group on a table's bill, and which of them are ready", false once
the pass shows group cards in columns (D91). Recommended, built meanwhile:

| | Text |
| --- | --- |
| English | Opens the pass: a card for each group a table sends, or each counter order, in columns from held to ready. |
| Spanish | Abre el pase: una tarjeta por cada grupo que envía una mesa, o por cada pedido de mostrador, en columnas de en espera a listo. |

FYI, no change recommended (review-1 M16): one pass screen will show «Curso listo» (`expo.ready`,
the Course Ready lever on a counter order's card) beside «Siguiente pase» (approved, the Next course
card label). The master asks never to use two Spanish words for one meaning; these are two
meanings, a menu course («Curso», as the till's course field says, `table.course_label`) and a
table's next round of firing («pase»), so both stay. The alternative is «Pase listo» for the lever.

**Question 5 (asked 2026-11-03 ~09:55, "LANE C 2026-11-03 ~09:55" in
`~/waitron-campaign-c/questions.md`; answered 2026-11-03 ~12:33, below; D94): the station's Held
column on a 1024 px tablet.** What the owner approved (A797,
Q42) gives the station's held dishes their own Held column. The station board switches from columns
side by side to one column at a time under tabs when it is narrower than its columns need: four
columns need 905 px of board at normal text size, five need 1148 px (D93). So a 1024 px tablet (an
iPad-size kitchen screen; its board is 944 px) and a 1280 px screen with large text would change from
columns to tabs the day B4b lands, even with nothing held; collapsing Held to a strip does not save
it (961 px against 944).

Worked example: the Grill tablet is 1024 px wide. Today it shows Overview, Waiting, Preparing and
Ready side by side. Table 6's dessert course is held.
- (a) Accept tabs: the Grill tablet always shows one column at a time, with tabs across the top,
  Held's included, held course or not.
- **(b) Recommended, built meanwhile (D93):** Held is drawn only while it holds a card. With nothing
  held the tablet looks exactly as today. While Table 6's dessert is held, Held appears and the
  1024 px tablet switches to tabs; when the dessert is released, Held goes and the columns come back.
  A wide screen (1280 px, normal text) keeps columns either way.
- (c) Held starts hidden on every station: held cards stay in Waiting, dashed as today, until
  someone shows Held from the station's Columns window. Today's layout everywhere, but it departs
  from Q42 by default.

Recommended (b): it keeps Q42 (held dishes get their own column) without changing any station with
nothing held, and only a narrow screen pays for it, only while something is held. What changes in
B1b1 for (a) or (c) is in D93; (c) also makes Question 3's approved help text false by default and
needs new words before B4b lands.

**Answered 2026-11-03 ~12:33** (relayed by the watcher), with none of (a)–(c): "i don't think we
should use tabs. i like the option of having the held column below waiting. but waiting and overview
(in fact all columns) are optional, so the user can choose what they want. so i think all columns
should be togglable, and one special to combine waiting and held". Asked what "no tabs" means, the
owner chose: Held never forces tabs; today's tab fallback stays only for a screen too narrow for the
columns switched on (a phone, say); by default Held sits combined under Waiting. Built as D94, which
supersedes D93; what is left open is Question 6.

**Question 6 (asked 2026-11-03, revision 5, reworded after the revision 5 review; answered
2026-11-03, below: Part 1 (a) and its words as recommended, built by B1d1a–B1d4; Part 2 (b), new
work, D95 and Tasks B1e1–B1e4): the station's columns when two of them hold the same cards, the
words that go with them, and whether the pass follows the same rule.**

**Part 1: the station.** Your answer to Question 5 is built as you put it. By default a station
shows Overview, Waiting, Preparing and Ready, and held cards sit at the bottom of Waiting under a
**Held** heading that appears only while something is held, so a 1024 px tablet looks as today,
held course or not. The screen turns to tabs only when the columns switched on do not fit. Every
column can be switched off, as today (with all of them off the screen keeps its header row and
Columns, so a column can be brought back).

How the plan read "one special to combine waiting and held": as a **column** of its own, a sixth
row in the Columns window with its own Show switch: Overview, **Waiting + Held** (on), **Waiting**
(off), **Held** (off), Preparing, Ready. Read as a **switch** instead ("show held cards under
Waiting"), it is option (c) below. Under the column reading, someone can switch on two columns that
hold the same cards, so what the board shows then has to be decided.

Worked example: the Grill tablet. Table 4's burger is waiting to be started; Table 6's dessert
course is held.
- Case 1: a cook switches **Held** on and leaves **Waiting + Held** on.
- Case 2: a cook switches **Waiting** on and leaves **Waiting + Held** on.
- Case 3: a cook switches **Waiting** on and **Waiting + Held** off, and leaves **Held** off.

- **(a) Recommended, built meanwhile: each column always shows what its name says, and nothing
  switches by itself.** Case 1: the dessert shows twice, under Held at the bottom of Waiting + Held
  and in the Held column, until someone switches Waiting + Held off. Case 2: two columns are both
  headed **Waiting** / «Esperando»: the combined one reads "Waiting · 2" (the burger, then Held · 1,
  the dessert), the separate one "Waiting · 1" (the burger again); the board heading does not say
  which is which, only the Columns window does. Case 3: the dessert has no card on the board;
  Overview still lists it as held, after the +, as a hidden column's cards are counted today.
- (b) Each card shows once. A held card goes to Held when Held is on, otherwise under Waiting +
  Held, otherwise to the bottom of Waiting; a waiting card goes to Waiting when it is on, otherwise
  to Waiting + Held. Case 1: the dessert only in Held. Case 2: the burger only in the separate
  Waiting, so the combined column shows only the dessert, still headed Waiting. Case 3: the dessert
  at the bottom of Waiting.
- (c) The switch reading: the Columns window keeps them apart for you. Switching Waiting or Held on
  switches Waiting + Held off, and switching Waiting + Held on switches Waiting and Held off. Cases
  1 and 2 cannot happen; case 3 is as in (a).

The words, under every choice: the Columns window names the combined column **Waiting + Held** /
«Esperando + En espera»; on the board it keeps today's heading **Waiting** / «Esperando», with
**Held** / «En espera» over its held cards. The station card's help (Question 3's approved text)
says a held card "is in the Held column", which is no longer true by default, and the Columns badge
says Columns "hides the ones you do not need", though two columns now start hidden. Recommended
replacements:

| | Station card (only the second and third sentences change) |
| --- | --- |
| English | Each card is a group a table sent, or a counter order. A card none of whose dishes has been sent to this station to be cooked is held, with a dashed border and no minutes: it sits under Held at the bottom of the Waiting column, or in the Held column if this screen shows one. It says Held, not released until a waiter requests cooking, and Waiting for the pass once the waiter has asked and the pass has not yet fired it. Every other card is in the column of its least advanced dish; its minutes count from when its first dish was sent to this station to be cooked, or moved here. Tap a dish to move it on one step (on a Whole ticket card, with every dish at its step), or the card's heading to move on the dishes holding it back. Its top edge turns from green to amber, then its whole border red, as the card waits, by the station's late flags. |
| Spanish | Cada tarjeta es un grupo que envió una mesa, o un pedido de mostrador. Una tarjeta sin ningún plato mandado a preparar a esta estación está en espera, con el borde discontinuo y sin minutos: aparece bajo En espera, al final de la columna Esperando, o en la columna En espera si esta pantalla la muestra. Dice En espera, sin marchar hasta que un camarero pida la preparación, y Esperando al pase cuando ya la ha pedido y el pase aún no la ha marchado. Las demás tarjetas están en la columna de su plato menos avanzado; sus minutos cuentan desde que su primer plato se mandó a preparar a esta estación, o se movió aquí. Toca un plato para avanzarlo un paso (en una tarjeta que dice Toda la comanda, con todos los platos de su paso), o la cabecera de la tarjeta para avanzar los platos que la retienen. Su borde superior pasa de verde a ámbar, y después todo el borde a rojo, mientras la tarjeta espera, según los avisos de demora de la estación. |

| | Columns badge (station and pass; only the second sentence changes) |
| --- | --- |
| English | Tap a column's heading to fold it to a narrow strip, and again to open it. Columns sets the order of the columns and shows or hides each one; some start hidden. This device keeps both, for each board. |
| Spanish | Toca la cabecera de una columna para plegarla en una franja estrecha, y otra vez para abrirla. Columnas ordena las columnas y muestra u oculta cada una; algunas empiezan ocultas. Este dispositivo guarda las dos cosas para cada tablero. |

**Part 2: the pass.** "Held never forces tabs" was said of the station. The pass, as it landed in
B4a (#2425), always shows Held, To fire, In progress and Ready, and four card columns need 960 px
of board. A 1024 px pass tablet's board is 944 px, so it shows one list instead of columns, held
course or not; a 1280 px screen shows the columns (measured by the revision 5 review, probe P6).
Worked example: the pass on a 1024 px tablet at service. Today it shows Table 6's held dessert and
Table 4's burger in one scrolling list; without the Held column, the other three columns would fit
side by side (about 717 px: 3 × 231 + 2 × 12, read from the same sizes).
- **(a) Recommended: keep the pass as it landed.** Its Columns window already lets a pass hide
  Held, and then the other three columns fit at 1024 px, though its held cards are then off the
  board. If you want the pass
  to keep Held without losing its columns at 1024 px, that becomes a follow-up in the backlog
  (for example, held cards at the bottom of To fire, as on the station).
- (b) Do it in this pull request: the pass gets the station's shape (a combined "Held + To fire"
  column by default, the separate Held a choice).
- (c) Start the pass with Held hidden, so a 1024 px pass shows three columns; its held cards are
  then on no part of the board until someone shows Held (the pass's Overview starts hidden too).

If you pick (b) or (c) in part 1, the station's rule moves into the board (b) or into the Columns
window (c); B1d3's overlap checks change and the guide's sentence about overlapping columns with
them. The words and the help texts are the same under all three. If you pick (b) or (c) in part 2,
new tasks are planned for the pass before B4b lands.

**Answered 2026-11-03**, in three relays in `~/waitron-campaign-c/questions.md` (quoted; the
owner's words reach this plan through the watcher):
- **Part 1, the station** ("WATCHER 2026-11-03 ~14:30"): "The owner answered (a) at ~14:10 to the
  station question as first worded: each column always shows what its name says, nothing switches
  by itself; the words and the station-card help text (EN + ES) as proposed." Built by B1d1a–B1d4 (`f0c7bf2c28`,
  `4419898684`, `3e3f537265`, `f7ad5732f0`, `b0e96167a9`; review fixes `d5eaf373a6`).
- **Case 2 and the Columns badge** ("WATCHER 2026-11-03 ~16:10"): "**Case 2** (the separate
  Waiting and the combined Waiting + Held both on, both headed «Esperando»): **fine as is** — no
  heading change; Columns shows which is which." And "**Columns badge**, second sentence, station
  and pass: **as proposed** — EN "Columns sets the order of the columns and shows or hides each
  one; some start hidden." / ES «Columnas ordena las columnas y muestra u oculta cada una; algunas
  empiezan ocultas.»" B1d4 built exactly that text (`help-strings.ts:53` and `:114`, read against
  the answer).
- **Part 2, the pass** ("WATCHER 2026-11-03 ~16:05"): "The owner chose (b): in B4b, give the pass
  the station's shape — a combined **Held + To fire** column by default (held cards at its bottom
  under a Held heading, as the station's Waiting + Held), the separate **Held** column a choice in
  Columns. Plan the new pass tasks before B4b lands; follow Part 1's (a) rule for overlapping
  columns on the pass too (each column shows what its name says, nothing switches by itself)."
  The owner first asked why the pass says "To fire" where the station says "Waiting"; the watcher
  explained they are different steps (To fire: the waiter asked and the pass has still to fire;
  Waiting: fired, the station has not started). Planned as D95, Tasks B1e1–B1e4.

No other new words: every other phrase B4 shows is on the approved sheet or already in the till or
dashboard with the same meaning (finding 12; D73, D84, D85, D87), apart from Question 6's and D95's

**Interfaces.**

```ts
// state/pass-view.ts
export type PassMode = "pass" | "monitor" | "all_stations";
export type ItemCaption =
  | { kind: "none" }
  | { kind: "waitingForPass"; station: string }
  | { kind: "otherPass"; station: string }
  | { kind: "state"; state: TicketState; station: string }
  | { kind: "alreadyAt"; state: TicketState; station: string };
export interface ItemView { item: PassCardItem; caption: ItemCaption; dimmed: boolean;
  band: TimingBand | null; forgotten: boolean; closed: "blocked" | "extra" | null }
export interface CardView { card: PassCard; column: "held" | "toFire" | "inProgress" | "ready";
  kindWord: string | null; ageFrom: string | null; ageWord: "minutes" | "ready" | null;
  band: TimingBand | null; items: ItemView[];
  fire: { shown: boolean; enabled: boolean };
  groupReady: boolean; courseReady: { orderId: string; courseId: string }[] }
export function passItemBand(item: PassCardItem, now: number): TimingBand | null; // D68: null when held
export function passCardViews(board: PassBoard, mode: PassMode, runsPass: boolean, now: number): Map<ColumnKey, CardView[]>;
export function passOverdueCount(views: Map<ColumnKey, CardView[]>): number; // cards whose band >= overdue
export function passOverviewDishes(board: PassBoard): OverviewDish[];
// state/kitchen-columns.ts
export const PASS_COLUMNS: ColumnState; // { order: overview, held, toFire, inProgress, ready; hidden: [overview]; collapsed: [] }
```

Strings (EN; ES from the approved sheet, word for word): `pass.column.held` Held/«En espera»,
`pass.column.to_fire` To fire/«Por marchar», `pass.column.in_progress` In progress/«En curso»,
`pass.column.ready` Ready/«Listo», `pass.kind.new_order` New order/«Nuevo pedido»,
`pass.kind.next_course` Next course/«Siguiente pase», `pass.kind.added` Added/«Añadido», `pass.fire`
Fire/«Marchar», `pass.waiting_for_pass` Waiting for the pass/«Esperando al pase», `pass.other_pass`
"{station}: waiting for its pass"/«{station}: esperando a su pase», `pass.already_at` "Already at
{station}"/«Ya en {station}», `pass.station_closed_dish` "{station} is closed: move the dish to
another station from the till, or open {station} for today"/«{station} está cerrada: cambia el plato
de estación desde el TPV o abre {station} por hoy», `pass.station_closed_extra` "{station} is closed:
Fire still sends it there"/«{station} está cerrada: Marchar lo envía allí igualmente». Tests read
these from the catalogue, never a pasted Spanish literal; that each `es` value matches the owner's
sheet word for word is a review step of Task A19 (the sheet lives outside the repository).

`pass-reprint` `{ orderId }`, `toggle-column` `{ key }` and `open-columns` `{ opener }` (the station
widget's names, `station-queue.ts`; driver decision). Reprint is drawn only when the `showReprint`

## Task A7: the new pass screen — reads, refresh, Columns (about 60 calls)

D70, D71, D72. **Files:** new `apps/till/src/screens/till-pass-screen.ts` (`till-pass-screen`) and
`till-pass-screen.test.ts`. Port, do not rewrite, the old screen's read machinery
(`till-expo-screen.ts:520-710`: choice read, refresh every 15 s with a 25 s limit, request
generations, stale line, `device-unauthorized`, `kitchen-screen-changed`, lost slots and nothing
left), changing only the reads: `"pass"` → `getDevicePassBoard`, `"monitor"` →
`getDevicePassMonitorBoard`, `"all"` → `getExpoBoard`. New properties `deviceId` and `serverOrigin`
(for the Columns scope), `initialBoard?: DevicePassBoard`. Keep every property, event and hook D71
lists, and the header today's screen draws (`till-expo-screen.ts:742-749`: the device's name as
title on a display; `expo.title` and Back, `data-back`, emitting `back-to-counter`, on a till), with
Columns at the end of that header row (review-1 M9; the coverage line is backlog). That row is the
board's: the screen puts its title and Back (or the device's name) in `till-pass-board`'s `lead`
slot, as the station screen fills `till-station-queue`'s, and the board draws its overdue count and
then Columns after them. The stale line, the reprint and lever errors and the lost-slot lines go in
the board's `status` slot, under that row, so the overdue count stays above the stale and error lines
as today (A6 review point 2); the lost-slot lines, above the overdue badge today, now follow it. The screen owns a
`TickingClock` and hands its time to the board as `now` (D68). Columns: read on connect from the
scope (`screen` per mode), save on every change, open the existing `till-kitchen-columns-dialog`
with the pass's names; storage failure leaves the in-memory state (A797 D4).

## Task B1b1: the station's Held column, drawn while it holds a card, and its checks (about 55 calls)

**Built** (`7cb91f7ec4`, review fix `52ea90099d`) on D93, which D94 supersedes (revision 5). Kept
as the record of what was built; B1d1a, B1d1b and B1d2 rework it. Not a task to run.

D78, D93 (built on Owner question 5's recommended (b), pending: this task is built now and reworked
as D93 says if the answer differs; B4b does not land before the answer). Revision 4 split the old
B1b (review I1). **Files:** `apps/till/src/state/kitchen-columns.ts` (`STATION_COLUMNS.order`
gains `held` after `overview`); `apps/till/src/widgets/station-queue.ts`; and the four test files of
B1b1's Changed test checks rows (`widgets/station-queue.test.ts`, `state/kitchen-columns.test.ts`,
`screens/till-station-screen.test.ts`, `widgets/kitchen-columns-dialog.test.ts`).

Held is not one more `DRAWN` entry (`station-queue.ts:81-85`): a held card's `column` is
`"queued"` (`state/kitchen-cards.ts`, `KitchenCard.column` is a `TicketState`), and `#tabs`
(`:1104-1147`) and `#board` (`:1149-1182`) filter by `card.column === state` (review I1). So the
widget maps each shown key to its cards (`held`: `since === null`; `waiting`: `column === "queued"`
and `since !== null`; `preparing` and `ready`: `column` equal, as today), its title
(`pass.column.held` for `held`, «En espera»; `station.state.*` otherwise), its section's
`data-column` (`"held"`; the others keep today's state values) and its tab (`data-column-tab="held"`).

## Left for PR C, PR D and later

**PR C** (master Task 10): the station editor's "Waits for the pass" («Espera al pase»), removing
B1 D9's `omit: ["waits_for_pass"]` with the import's boolean validation, and the coverage warning.
After B4 a venue that switches waiting on gets a working pass, station, table screen and dashboard.

**PR D** (master Tasks 11–12): ticket headings, "Goes with kitchen food", removing `away_at`,
`pass_item_marks` (its last writers are the two `pass-board.test.ts` cases A13a moves, "legacy Done
marks stay device-specific but never hide food from either pass" and "A773 no dispatch state:
splitting keeps the source mark without copying it or hiding food"; find them by title) and
`fire_control`.

**Later (backlog entries B4's last tasks add or keep):**
- Help badges for the pass's columns, To fire and Fire, and the station's Held column (new words).
- Short names on pass cards (A797 slice 12b; Overview is built here).
- `device.forbidden_action`'s "Ask a manager to change it." sends the person to someone else (D87).
- Department transfers say "Waiting" for a held dish (D81).
- The till's `ScreenSlot` lacks the server's `switchedOff` (B3.7 Minor 1; not needed by B4).
- Kept from B3: a pass warning before releasing to a station whose output is down (D54); an edit
  adding an extra at a waiting station prints no `+N` on its HOLD paper; one pass-board rule no test
  holds (a listed but switched-off zone, `passScopeOf`; the course-section half retires with D76,
  review-1 M10); the slip order of a several-station move.
- The pass header's line naming what the pass covers (A797 §3.5; new words).
- The manager overview's "orders taking too long" ages a dish waiting for the pass, and a held one,
  from its insert (`packages/reporting/src/overdue-orders.ts`; review-1 M11).
- A1250 (the pass's warm colour) re-pointed to `widgets/pass-board.ts`.
